# Rules digest

2040 rule statements from 257 videos. Each is filed under one subject and tagged with every concept it names. The concept counts, not the sentence counts, are what show emphasis: the same instruction is written a different way in every video.

Every rule and figure links to the video and the second it was
        stated; where a rule is stated in several videos the link is to the
        clearest phrasing.

Generated by `scripts/pipeline/07_extract_rules.py`.

## stock-selection (268 rules)

### news catalyst (83)

- Step 3: Define stock selection strategy with five criteria: (1) price $1-$20, (2) up 10%+ on day, (3) 5x higher volume than average, (4) news event, (5) float under 10 million shares. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @16:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=975s)
- Volume criterion: look for 5x relative volume; indicator of news catalyst bringing in volume. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @22:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1374s)
- News event: catalyst driving the move; required for momentum trading. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @23:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1394s)
- Trade primarily between 8:00 a.m. and 10:00 a.m., when volume and news-driven moves peak. Volume starts at 7:00 a.m.; after 10:30 a.m. market cools. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @2:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=155s)
- Require five criteria for a high-probability setup: price between 2–20, news catalyst, high relative volume, stock up at least 10%, float under 20 million shares. Do not force trades. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @10:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=647s)
- When news breaks, look for micro pullback and buy the dip; apply tight stop at the low of the pullback. — [`0Zh105lq9Rk` @7:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0Zh105lq9Rk&t=421s)
- Stock selection rule: % up minimum 10%, price $0.75–$10, relative volume 5x+, news catalyst, float under 20M shares (ideally under 10M). — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @31:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=1865s)
- Five pillars of stock selection: price, float, catalyst/news, relative volume, percentage gain (all must align). — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @22:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=1372s)
- Five criteria for best stocks to trade: (1) up at least 10%, (2) five times relative volume, (3) news/catalyst, (4) price $2-$20 (sweet spot $5-$10), (5) float under 20 million shares. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @6:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=412s)
- Scanner criteria: stocks up 10%+, high relative volume (5x = 500% normal volume), price between $1-20, float under 10 million shares, positive news catalyst. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @20:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=1243s)
- Small stock that moves from $20-$30 down to $1-$2 on news bounce can easily hit 50-100-200% gains in one day. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @2:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=120s)
- Scanner criteria: stocks up 10%+ in short period; look for news catalyst at 8:30 a.m. or during market. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @3:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=186s)
- Entry: break of highs after pullback on news-driven momentum. — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @22:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1352s)
- Focus on stocks gapping higher at market open (result of after-hours news). — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @35:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=2146s)
- Only trade on news-driven catalysts (biotech clinical trials, FDA approvals, earnings, etc.). — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @27:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1641s)
- Require 5x above-average relative volume to indicate something special is happening (catalyst-driven). — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @19:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=1176s)
- Low float stock (e.g., 4.28 million shares) with fresh catalyst can move quickly and break resistance. — [`8h1xWOkgrpI` @2:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8h1xWOkgrpI&t=173s)
- News catalyst must exist; without news, risk is higher. — [`8h1xWOkgrpI` @3:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8h1xWOkgrpI&t=210s)
- News catalyst: required for small account, preferred but not required for regular account. — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @11:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=677s)
- After breaking news catalyst, first pullback should hold at least 50% of initial leg up; green candle followed by first candle making new high confirms reversal. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @50:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=3034s)
- Trade during first hour of market (9:30-10:30 AM) when market reacts to overnight news. — [`BaZ4R2ovI9k` @4:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BaZ4R2ovI9k&t=247s)
- Catalyst (news event) must be present. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @2:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=176s)
- On earnings catalyst: buy on the first micro pullback with a tight stop when stock breaks to new high. — [`DAnpW98ZCSg` @8:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DAnpW98ZCSg&t=525s)
- Five pillars of quality setup: stock price $2-20, float under 20M shares, up 10%+ today, new fundamental catalyst, high relative volume. — [`DJ9QEMbTdfA` @2:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DJ9QEMbTdfA&t=143s)
- Prefer news catalyst as driver of stock movement; catalyst creates demand for the move. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @21:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=1307s)
- Stock selection criteria: news catalyst preferred, but not required in hot markets. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @18:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1084s)
- Look for stocks up 5% or more to identify catalyst momentum before looking for news. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @35:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2103s)
- T1 halt = stock halted pending news; usually means news is bad (company requested halt). — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @1:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=119s)
- T1 pending news halt: company usually resumes at ~50% of current price. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @24:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=1469s)
- Prefer news catalyst, especially buzzword (AI, blockchain, etc.) to support move. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @11:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=719s)

### volume confirmation (46)

- Relative volume must be at least 5x the 50-day average. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @26:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=1617s)
- Volume rotation: with small float but massive volume (e.g., 800,000 shares float, 43 million volume), shares trade many times (float rotation). — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @14:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=869s)
- Lookfor 5x relative volume (5 times above average daily volume) as key indicator. — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @22:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1367s)
- Stock must have more than 25 million shares volume today for optimal trading conditions. — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @22:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1343s)
- Require stocks to have five times above-average relative volume as baseline filter. — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @20:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=1244s)
- For low float stocks on light volume, limit position size to 2,000-5,000 shares to avoid excessive risk. — [`9VAKgj1Px3s` @4:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VAKgj1Px3s&t=243s)

- Relative volume must be at least five times average. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @2:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=152s)
- Require minimum 5x relative volume (trading 5x the 50-day average volume) as signal of exceptional event. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @18:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=1086s)
- Stock selection criteria: up at least 10% on day with 5x relative volume. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @17:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1079s)
- Focus scanner on low-float stocks with high relative volume (5x or higher). — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @35:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2115s)
- Target stocks with 5x or higher relative volume compared to 50-day average. — [`G4uTdr_zx1I` @22:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=G4uTdr_zx1I&t=1371s)
- Need relative volume 5x or higher; if not, wait for setup to improve. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @8:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=495s)
- Relative volume must be at least five times average, indicating strong demand. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @9:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=547s)

- Relative volume: at least 5x normal daily volume (5:1 ratio minimum). — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @20:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=1208s)
- Trade stocks with five times relative volume today. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @4:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=276s)
- Relative volume: Looking for 5x above average volume for high-relative-volume setups. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @7:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=434s)

### float size (47)

- Float criterion: under 10 million shares available; restricts supply creating momentum. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @23:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1395s)
- Float changes during split: pre-split 20 million shares ÷ 20 = 1 million post-split. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @12:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=734s)
- Maximum float: prefer under 5 million shares; even under 1 million is favorable. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @13:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=819s)
- Trade stocks with float under 10 million shares for larger percentage moves. — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @30:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1812s)
- Float criteria: Prefer under 50 million shares; under 25 million better; under 10 million better; under 5 million best [00:15:45] — [`7RbjGk9eNJU` @7:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7RbjGk9eNJU&t=470s)
- Require stocks with float under 10 million shares. — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @24:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=1454s)
- Float: must be under 20 million shares absolute maximum; under 5 million preferred for small accounts. — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @11:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=675s)
- Float must be 1.3 million shares or lower for strong moves (example: DTSS had 1.3M float). — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @3:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=205s)
- Filter for float under 20 million shares; lower float = higher probability of large percentage gains. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @22:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=1335s)
- Float threshold: do not trade stocks above 100 million shares float; prefer below 30 million, ideally below 10-20 million. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @33:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=1995s)
- Stocks with float under 10 million shares are more likely to make large percentage moves. — [`G4uTdr_zx1I` @16:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=G4uTdr_zx1I&t=987s)
- Float under 10 million shares; can go up to 20M but not preferred. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @12:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=742s)
- Float must be under 20 million shares, creating supply constraint. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @9:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=589s)
- For bullish dip trades, prefer stocks with low float and strong chart pattern; allows better chance of getting out green. — [`M07xElrhVYM` @13:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=782s)
- Prefer stocks under 30 million float, specifically 10-20 million float range performed best early on. — [`M07xElrhVYM` @15:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=902s)
- Focus on stocks between $1 and $10 with float under 10 million shares, up at least 25% on the day. — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @11:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=671s)
- Float: under 20 million shares (under 10M preferred); aim for less than 1M for strongest moves. — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @18:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=1086s)
- Float less than 20 million shares. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @15:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=910s)

### pre-market activity (34)

- Alternative windows: 9:30am-4:00pm (regular hours); 3:30pm-5:00pm (power hour); 4:00am-7:00am (premarket). — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @5:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=303s)
- Use mental stops in pre-market (before live halts in regular hours). [00:51:36] Stop at low of pullback candle. — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @47:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=2824s)
- Gap-and-go setup: break of pre-market high during market open = entry signal. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @8:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=495s)
- Extended hours trading: pre-market (4:00 a.m.) through regular (9:30 a.m.-4:00 p.m.) through after-hours (until 8:00 p.m.). [05:42] — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @5:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=308s)
- Market orders: Only available 9:30 a.m. - 4:00 p.m. EST; pre-market and after-hours require limit orders. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @23:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=1436s)

- Pre-market chart analysis: if stock pops up then fades back down, caution; fading = weak setup. — [`8h1xWOkgrpI` @1:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8h1xWOkgrpI&t=100s)
- With Thinkorswim pre-market, cannot use market orders; must use buy-ask and sell-bid combination. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @11:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=666s)

- Stop orders not allowed pre-market or after hours; only limit orders permitted. — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @4:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=284s)
- Cannot place market orders pre-market or after hours; must use limit orders only. — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @43:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=2581s)
- Pre-market trading allows use of leverage (unlike after-hours). — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @26:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=1600s)
- Daily charts should not show pre-market or after-hours highs/lows; only regular trading hours candles standard. — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @38:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=2308s)
- Pre-market session: 4:00am to 9:30am EST; regular hours: 9:30am to 4:00pm; after-hours: 4:00pm to 8:00pm EST. — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @3:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=187s)
- Most retail brokers do not allow trading before 7:00am; 7am-9:30am is most active pre-market window. — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @18:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=1126s)
- Use live stop-loss orders when possible during regular trading hours (not available pre-market). — [`RoAVk--vljU` @16:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RoAVk--vljU&t=995s)

- Trick 7: Always enable pre-market data on intraday charts or indicators will be incorrect. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @40:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2442s)
- Block calendar 9:30am-11am for pre-market trading window when employment allows schedule flexibility. — [`bnnEm_DKwXE` @2:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bnnEm_DKwXE&t=152s)
- Check pre-market action: if spike up followed by dump, signals big seller coming in after market open. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @1:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=78s)
- At market open (9:30 a.m.): if stock dumps despite pre-market strength, indicates overhead seller. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @1:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=96s)
- Trend line web theory: if you draw trend lines connecting multiple candlestick wicks (premarket through day), reactions occur at those levels consistently; suggests market maker orders stacked there. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @25:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1536s)
- Pre-market screening: run gap scanners at 9:20 a.m.; filter for stocks up more than 5%. — [`dl1MtlsMoZw` @8:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dl1MtlsMoZw&t=507s)
- Only trade stocks gapping up at least 2% in pre-market before the opening bell. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @14:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=844s)
- Stop orders work only between 9:30 a.m. and 4:00 p.m.; pre-market and after-hours trading requires limit orders due to higher volatility and no market orders. — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @5:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=333s)
- Entry on gap-and-go: break above pre-market high level (SQL broke $1.30, targeting $1.40+). — [`nm-rhysU96k` @3:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nm-rhysU96k&t=218s)
- Use limit orders, not market orders, for pre-market trading. — [`oKlhUSSHe2Q` @60:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=oKlhUSSHe2Q&t=3616s)
- Use trailing stops only in regular market hours (after 9:30am ET), not pre-market. — [`pYx756KSvR4` @31:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pYx756KSvR4&t=1862s)
- For pre-market and halt resumptions, monitor tape without stop orders (pre-market doesn't support stop orders). — [`q5DRctM5C-Q` @42:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q5DRctM5C-Q&t=2570s)
- Do not use stop orders in pre-market (not available) or on small caps during regular hours (stop hunting risk). — [`s7kEHvd0TJk` @16:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=s7kEHvd0TJk&t=985s)
- _...4 more._

### scanner alert (13)

- Use tight stops on scanner trades; scale in on strength. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @1:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=101s)
- High-day momentum scanner trade entry: watch for alert, set buy on offer, set sell on bid or offer. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @2:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=127s)
- Three-step process: (1) find stock via scanner, (2) analyze chart for technical setup, (3) pull up Level 2 to validate entry before executing trade. — [`4syXgXshgsc` @50:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=3022s)
- Primary scanner: any stock gapping up or down >4%. — [`8h1xWOkgrpI` @0:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8h1xWOkgrpI&t=41s)
- Stock must hit real-time scanner as first qualification step before further evaluation. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @26:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=1595s)
- Decision fatigue accumulates with each scanner alert throughout day; by noon, accuracy declines significantly. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @29:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=1750s)
- When scanner alerts on stock, pull up ticker and check if already familiar (previous reverse split discussion in community). — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @1:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=106s)
- Look for signs of strength: green on the tape, decreasing share orders on level two, natural price increase, new highs on high day scanner. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @31:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1860s)
- Looking for stocks already up at least 10% as minimum before entering scanner-based trades. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @5:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=350s)
- Daily breakout entry: stock hits scanner near high-of-day ($18.25 area); immediately pull chart and identify daily trigger at $18.86. — [`gA8r-ZoN49Q` @4:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gA8r-ZoN49Q&t=298s)
- Minimum stock move to trigger scanner alert: up more than 10% within 5 minutes. — [`iIC62xnblLc` @9:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iIC62xnblLc&t=543s)
- Use RSI divergence scan (custom scanner) to find stocks at extreme RSI levels automatically. — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @22:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=1329s)
- When multiple stocks at similar prices appear on scanner, verify order before pressing buy; not doing so is a "rookie mistake." — [`q_ZliMvD8yc` @0:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q_ZliMvD8yc&t=22s)

### reversal (2)

- Stock must maintain price above $1 per share to remain on NASDAQ or NYSE; below $1 triggers delisting notice and forces reverse split. — [`SfkJNbFUg0c` @4:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SfkJNbFUg0c&t=242s)
- Stock must be up at least 10% already; only trade momentum, not reversals. — [`xgnqOu_fchA` @21:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xgnqOu_fchA&t=1265s)

### support and resistance (2)

- Check daily chart before adding to watch list; look for 200 MA as potential resistance. — [`8h1xWOkgrpI` @1:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8h1xWOkgrpI&t=82s)
- Entry pattern criteria: Buy first candle making new high after pullback from resistance level. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @32:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1928s)

### gap and go (2)

- When leading gapper fails at open and doesn't hold highs, slow down and be more cautious. — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @6:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=360s)
- Sympathy momentum doesn't always shift to secondary stocks; usually everything gets choppy when leading gapper fails. — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @6:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=374s)

### A-quality setup only (2)

- Be selective in cold markets; focus on quality setups; in hot markets, can be less selective. — [`HY7i78bOJFA` @11:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HY7i78bOJFA&t=682s)
- Accuracy focus: Only trade A-quality setups that meet all five criteria; B and C-quality setups degrade accuracy and profit-to-loss ratio. [00:40:34] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @56:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3395s)

### accuracy / win rate (2)

- Taking larger size on fewer, higher-conviction setups forces selectivity and improves accuracy. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @46:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2799s)
- Focus on accuracy and quality stock selection as the starting point of a positive feedback loop. — [`V9za4wt9ONo` @8:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V9za4wt9ONo&t=501s)

### VWAP (3)

- Stock must hold above VWAP during dip pullback. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @28:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=1689s)
- Stock must maintain bullish chart structure overall (above 9 EMA, above 20 EMA, above VWAP) even during dip. — [`M07xElrhVYM` @11:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=712s)
- Setup requirement: Stock must have squeezed up at least 10% initially before pulling back below VWAP. [00:05:27] — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @1:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=105s)

### uncategorised (32)

- Price criterion: focus $2-$10 for best profitability; avoid stocks under $1 or over $10. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @20:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1254s)
- Stock selection for Phase 2: top three leading percentage gainers with criteria applied strictly. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @34:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2068s)
- Stock must be in the top 5% of leading percentage gainers in the market for this setup to work. [00:14:06] — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @1:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=99s)
- Stock must be up at least 30% on the day to be considered (minimum threshold for entry). — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @17:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=1020s)
- Stock must be up at least 10% on day, breaking standard deviation of typical stock volatility. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @2:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=143s)
- SPAC trading: recognize SPAC structure (blank-check company) and its hot trading history; use same selection criteria as other stocks. — [`DJ9QEMbTdfA` @5:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DJ9QEMbTdfA&t=359s)
- Stock selection criteria: price range 2-20 dollars per share. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @17:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1075s)
- Stock selection criteria: prefer floats under 20 million shares (lower better for exaggerated squeezes). — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @18:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1098s)
- Stock must be up at least 10% before considering trade. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @8:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=525s)
- Stock must break the 9 EMA to hold as potential dip trade; break below signals bears in control. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @28:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=1684s)
- Stock must be priced between $2 and $20 to see the biggest percentage moves. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @9:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=542s)
- Stock must be up at least 10% on the day, breaking standard deviation and indicating strong moves. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @9:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=566s)
- Use technical analysis such as Bollinger Bands, relative strength index, or moving averages for stock selection. — [`N3E5eYS8d-I` @4:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=N3E5eYS8d-I&t=276s)
- Monitor your watch list. — [`N3E5eYS8d-I` @6:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=N3E5eYS8d-I&t=414s)
- Stock must be up at least 10% on the day. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @15:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=909s)
- Stock must be up over 10% on the day versus the prior day's closing price. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0` @79:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0&t=4742s)
- Scan for stocks meeting these criteria at market open. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @6:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=370s)
- Focus on stocks meeting five pillars of stock selection [00:13:45] — [`ZRRCyIoZnHE` @13:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZRRCyIoZnHE&t=822s)
- A stock must be up at least 10% on the day to be considered volatile enough to trade. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @9:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=545s)
- Smart routing: broker algorithm selects best execution route automatically; not necessary to manually choose exchanges. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @24:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=1494s)
- Sophisticated traders can manually select specific routes (NASDAQ, ARCA, BATS) for execution. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @25:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=1537s)

- Pullback requirement: Stock must hold at least 50% of the initial move up; deeper retrace means weaker setup. — [`hLn6LrlXgAE` @13:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLn6LrlXgAE&t=826s)
- Minimum gap requirement: stock must be up at least 10%. — [`qlhkAdaOg_Q` @15:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qlhkAdaOg_Q&t=930s)
- Stock selection criterion 2: stock must be up at least 10% on the day. — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @24:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=1486s)
- Stock selection criterion 4: price range $1–$20 preferred (affordable for retail small accounts). — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @25:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=1513s)

## entry (331 rules)

### first candle to make a new high (58)

- Micro-pullback entry: stock squeezes up, pulls back on light volume, resumes higher; enter first candle making new high after pullback. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @27:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=1623s)
- First candle to make a new high after pullback is entry signal. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @33:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=1984s)
- Entry is first candle to make new high after pullback to 9-period EMA on one-minute chart. — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @14:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=861s)
- Enter on first candle to make a new high after a pullback in uptrend with MACD positive. — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @31:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=1881s)
- Enter on the first candle that makes a new high after a pullback (confirmation entry). — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @4:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=243s)
- Entry on bottoming tail candle when it makes a new high of day; target is high of day. — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @29:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=1769s)
- Buy on first candle that makes a new high after the pullback; stop at low of pullback. — [`Ai6r6xjk8Uk` @3:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ai6r6xjk8Uk&t=212s)
- Candle-over-candle pattern: first candle makes a new high versus previous candle = entry signal; max loss is the low of the pullback; profit target is a move back to the high of day. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @47:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=2840s)
- [00:10:44-00:10:48] Buy first candle that makes new high on pullback, or buy break of previous resistance — [`D8Guwf84eAA`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=D8Guwf84eAA)
- Entry: buy first candle to make new high after pullback. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @1:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=87s) _(2 videos)_
- First 5-minute candle to make a new high = breakout entry trigger. — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @3:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=180s)
- Treat first candle that makes new high after pullback as potential entry signal. — [`G4uTdr_zx1I` @25:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=G4uTdr_zx1I&t=1506s)
- Buy first candle to make new high after dips (candle over candle pattern). — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @14:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=871s)
- [00:08:25-00:08:26] Entry: first daily candle to make new high — [`Hxjla5IZYbA`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Hxjla5IZYbA)
- [00:03:35-00:03:51] Entry: first candle to make new high out of pattern, or break through point B high — [`IHeJROuEzRU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IHeJROuEzRU)
- First candle making new high on micro pullback is the entry trigger. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @48:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=2914s)
- Buy the first candle to make a new high after a pullback (candle over candle breakout). — [`L16yPlr8jyM` @5:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L16yPlr8jyM&t=343s)
- Entry technique: buy breakout of first candle to make new high (multi-candle pullback pattern). — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @28:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=1727s)
- First candle to make a new high is the entry. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @20:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1233s)
- Cold market strategy (opposite of hot): focus on breakout trading, not dip buying; buy first candle making new high, exit quickly. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @24:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1477s)
- [00:09:26-00:09:29] Buy first candle to make new high at psychological levels ($6, $6.50, $7, etc.) — [`S3vYOE9d6nU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=S3vYOE9d6nU)
- Enter the first candle that makes a new high after the pullback has held VWAP and shown support. — [`SCy6RrASpJY` @4:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SCy6RrASpJY&t=252s)
- Wait for micro pullback, buy at approximately previous high, anticipate break to new high. — [`SpnidshD8Zk` @9:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SpnidshD8Zk&t=573s)
- Entry: first candle to make new high above prior high after pullback. — [`UNoPqBWuLC0` @3:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UNoPqBWuLC0&t=232s)
- Wait for candle to make new high after pullback = signal to enter. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @12:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=775s)
- Enter on the first candle that makes a new high after pullback confirmation. — [`YiMItz4fgFk` @4:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YiMItz4fgFk&t=252s)
- Buy the first candle that makes a new high after pullback in a bull flag pattern. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @3:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=230s)
- Enter on the first candle that makes a new high after the pullback. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @4:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=252s) _(3 videos)_
- Enter on the first candle that breaks above the previous candle's high (candle-over-candle pattern). — [`_4Q0hlNFn-A` @23:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4Q0hlNFn-A&t=1382s)
- On ABCD pattern, wait for break of previous resistance level (not first candle to make new high) before full-size entry. — [`_4Q0hlNFn-A` @41:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4Q0hlNFn-A&t=2471s)

### support and resistance (46)

- Exit or avoid when price breaks above resistance on high volume but immediately dumps back down (rejection candle). — [`1FKu4LH0Xss` @4:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1FKu4LH0Xss&t=246s)
- Flat top breakout: buy breakout over resistance, especially with volume and moving average support. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @34:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=2078s)
- Buy near moving average support (9 EMA first, then 20, then 200). — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @27:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=1638s)
- Buy first pullback after initial breakout when MACD is positive and volume profile supports buying (green candles > red candles). — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @6:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=368s)
- Buy on support at micro pullbacks following breakouts above resistance. — [`BFH-0N8S-IA` @28:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BFH-0N8S-IA&t=1733s)
- Entry close to support minimizes stop loss, maximizing risk-reward ratio. — [`BFH-0N8S-IA` @14:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BFH-0N8S-IA&t=885s)
- Buy at reversal points where price dips lower (potential support areas) using patterns like hammers and candle-over-candle formations; sell at peaks where price has resistance. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @43:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=2631s)
- When price holds above critical support (e.g., $53-54), anticipate breakout above prior high (e.g., $55 resistance). — [`BaZ4R2ovI9k` @17:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BaZ4R2ovI9k&t=1046s)
- Analyze daily chart first for resistance levels, then look for intraday entries. — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @28:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=1718s)
- Trifecta for dip entry: (1) pullback to psychological support (whole dollar), (2) pullback to 9 EMA, (3) light volume pullback. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @23:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1407s)
- Buy at psychological support (e.g., $7.00) with stop at next support level (e.g., $6.99); look for move to $7.50, $8.00, $8.50, $9.00. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @25:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1504s)
- Swing trading: enter as close to support as possible to minimize max loss. — [`ICbPz3iXbG0` @19:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ICbPz3iXbG0&t=1160s)
- Best trades have entries at support with stops just below support for best risk-to-reward ratio. — [`ICbPz3iXbG0` @21:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ICbPz3iXbG0&t=1261s)
- Use breakout strategy: buy as stock breaks through resistance of prior high after pullback. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @13:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=811s)
- Scale into micro pullbacks: buy initial dip, add on subsequent pullbacks if price holds support. — [`MTbYDUswdbM` @2:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MTbYDUswdbM&t=177s)
- When seeing a big buy order appear on your chart, that creates support because that order is so large. — [`OQLfHuO8KlI` @7:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OQLfHuO8KlI&t=426s)
- Support level at $5.20 based on visible buy orders on the depth map. — [`OQLfHuO8KlI` @24:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OQLfHuO8KlI&t=1453s)
- Buy support, which can be ascending or descending resistance lines, horizontal levels, half dollars, whole dollars, VWAP, or moving averages. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @2:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=152s)
- For small dips: buy at support level with expectation of immediate reclaim of that level; use tight stop at the low of pullback. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @5:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=337s)
- Dip trading entry: take starter position at support (e.g., $6), stop at support level just below (e.g., $5.80). — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @21:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1278s)
- Nine moving average: stock support should be reasonably close; if 9 MA is too far below, not a good entry. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @26:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1604s)
- Do not buy at very top; be willing to skip trades if risk is too high; avoid buying at absolute resistance highs. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @36:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=2204s)
- Enter on breakout of resistance level with volume confirmation; volume must be higher than average. — [`RoAVk--vljU` @6:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RoAVk--vljU&t=379s)
- [00:08:45-00:09:38] Buy off ascending trend line support using trend line drawn through pullback lows — [`S3vYOE9d6nU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=S3vYOE9d6nU)
- Entry is triggered on the first candle breaking a support level after a pullback (ABCD pattern example at 22 cents). — [`SLMXOd13uqI` @4:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SLMXOd13uqI&t=263s)
- Check position of 200 EMA before considering entry—if stock is below it, it will likely run into resistance there. — [`YZ5nvP7DnQQ` @14:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YZ5nvP7DnQQ&t=899s)
- Do not buy at the very top of a move; manage risk by getting in near support. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @45:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=2700s)
- Never buy into resistance created by large sell orders on the ask without waiting for that order to be absorbed or canceled. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @15:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=935s)
- Step 3: Execute trades using multiple timeframes; buy dips that hold previous resistance as support; stop at previous swing low. — [`_RRClNoHamw` @31:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_RRClNoHamw&t=1874s)
- Entry on whole dollars and half-dollars: buy at psychological support levels. — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @29:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=1792s)
- _...16 more._

### volume confirmation (18)

- Break of volume-weighted average price with cup-and-handle consolidation below VWAP = entry trigger. — [`016ibIK58t0` @7:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=016ibIK58t0&t=467s)
- Once MACD crosses over and volume profile becomes choppy with higher volume red candles, stop trading the long side. — [`1FKu4LH0Xss` @34:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1FKu4LH0Xss&t=2077s)
- Buy pullbacks, dips, and breakouts on quality high-volume stocks. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @10:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=620s)
- High-volume red candles indicate selling pressure; avoid entry when this dominates. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @10:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=600s)
- Micro pullback entries: look for clean pullback on decreasing volume, then buy breakout of pullback high. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @25:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1550s)
- Volume confirmation: increasing volume on pushes higher is buy signal; volume should increase during breakout attempt. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @31:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1917s)
- Technical analysis rule: high volume red rejection candle at top is exit signal; no re-entry after high-volume rejection. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @37:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=2277s)
- Never ignore: volume on candlestick charts (green/red volume bars showing buy/sell pressure). — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @68:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=4090s)
- For huge dips: buy into large red candle (one of top 10 biggest of the day) when panic flush has bottomed, with green volume confirmation on tape. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @12:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=768s)
- Do not buy stocks that have been beaten down and are still trading sideways; wait for them to start bouncing with volume and momentum first. — [`SfkJNbFUg0c` @0:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SfkJNbFUg0c&t=25s)
- Buy beaten-up stock entry when price breaks above VWAP (volume-weighted average price) with volume confirmation. — [`SfkJNbFUg0c` @0:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SfkJNbFUg0c&t=41s)
- Candle over candle with light selling volume = buyer confirmation; strong buy signal. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @52:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=3175s)
- Enter on the first pullback after a gap up using a bull flag formation. On higher volume spike, wait for pullback; do not chase straight out of the gate. — [`VNsh-8cS2Tg` @1:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VNsh-8cS2Tg&t=119s)
- After entry, expect to see large green candles, high volume buying, and price moving higher. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @19:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=1190s)
- Entry on resume from halt if stock breaks halt high with volume. — [`ag5JoiOlUy4` @1:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ag5JoiOlUy4&t=92s)
- Reversal candle (high-volume red after push up) = marks high; acts as signal to avoid further long entries or flip short. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @23:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1406s)
- SPAC entry: break of high-of-day level when volume starts to sustain upward movement. Example: break of $21.50, added at $22. — [`jpPgEAAVMxY` @2:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jpPgEAAVMxY&t=121s)
- Buy pullbacks when MACD is open/positive; sell when MACD goes negative or volume drops. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @22:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=1328s)

### micro pullback (16)

- Focus on micro pullback strategy: buy the first pullback in a strong uptrend when MACD is positive. — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @39:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=2369s)
- Use traditional candlestick charts for finding entry points on micro pullbacks; use Heikin-Ashi side-by-side as reference. — [`Ai6r6xjk8Uk` @3:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ai6r6xjk8Uk&t=186s)
- Enter on micro pullbacks during breakouts (1-minute chart entries after 5-minute chart setup). — [`BFH-0N8S-IA` @8:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BFH-0N8S-IA&t=484s)
- Entry strategy: wait for stock to pop up and pullback; buy the first micro pullback, not initial pop. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @25:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1530s)
- Buy micro pullbacks during strong moves (look for very small red candles). — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @22:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=1338s)
- Entry on micro pullback: buy during the brief pullback, expecting continuation up; typical target $0.50-$1.00 higher. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @28:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1685s)
- Dip trade with micro pullback strategy — Buy pullbacks within uptrends for quick scalps. — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @15:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=912s)
- Preferred strategy: wait for stock to pop, pause for micro pullback, then buy the dip for next leg higher (micro pullback strategy). — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @16:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=1002s)
- Micro pullback entry: buy when the next green candle breaks above the high of the preceding red candle. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @52:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=3140s)
- Entry technique: buy micro pullbacks (small dips during uptrend) for next leg higher. — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @17:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=1020s)
- Buy pullbacks on momentum waves; wait for micro-pullback before entering, then ride the next leg higher. — [`dl1MtlsMoZw` @10:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dl1MtlsMoZw&t=646s)
- Use pullback pattern and micro pullback entries on one-minute charts during uptrend resumption. — [`l7M03OXCIdA` @27:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=l7M03OXCIdA&t=1665s)
- Micro pullback strategy: stock squeezes up, has momentary pullback, then squeezed higher. Enter on the pullback; highest-consistency pattern. (One-minute timeframe). — [`m12FrF4rrOg` @10:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m12FrF4rrOg&t=638s)
- Enter on micro pullbacks to moving averages; for strong trends, sell on the ask to get best prices, buy on the ask when stock is squeezing up. — [`q5DRctM5C-Q` @35:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q5DRctM5C-Q&t=2100s)
- Entry strategy 1: micro pullback on front side of breakout (buy the dip for next leg up). — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @26:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=1606s)
- Micro pullbacks, bull flags, and ABCD patterns are strongest entry setups. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @9:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=540s)

### reversal (14)

- Buy stocks near all-time lows that have just completed a recent reverse split; this sets up explosive moves. — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @14:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=872s)
- Reversal confirmation: When price breaks outside bands, wait for next candle to make new low/high (not immediate entry). — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @6:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=362s)
- Trend trade (buy up, short down) rather than reversal trade; trend trading produces more success. — [`ICbPz3iXbG0` @22:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ICbPz3iXbG0&t=1328s)
- Long body candles getting bigger = accumulation of buying; long body candles getting smaller = trend exhaustion and reversal signal. — [`L16yPlr8jyM` @1:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L16yPlr8jyM&t=115s)
- Pull back, get reversal candles, then buy first candle over candle formation for entry. — [`L16yPlr8jyM` @22:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L16yPlr8jyM&t=1349s)
- Buy the dip when stock drops after popping above a technical level (half-dollar, whole-dollar); if first time, usually reverses after quick selling. — [`M07xElrhVYM` @9:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=547s)
- Do not enter on red candle; exit if reversal occurs after entry. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @32:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1964s)
- Long-legged doji (cross with upper and lower wicks) at top of run = potential reversal but requires confirmation. — [`Ymzyd9fbsmI` @4:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ymzyd9fbsmI&t=241s)
- For beginners doing reversal trading: focus on 10-15+ consecutive green/red candles, then short/long on first reversal candle. — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @20:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=1225s)
- Hammer candlestick = long lower wick at bottom of sell-off, close above or at open; bullish reversal. — [`kQ93wnMCMWQ` @4:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kQ93wnMCMWQ&t=267s)
- A candlestick with long upper wick and small body signals reversal or topping, especially after a big move up. — [`m12FrF4rrOg` @3:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m12FrF4rrOg&t=180s)
- A candlestick with long lower wick and small body signals reversal or bottoming, especially after a big move down. — [`m12FrF4rrOg` @3:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m12FrF4rrOg&t=223s)
- Morning star pattern (daily chart): stock sells off and gaps lower on next day, then opens lower but rallies with hammer formation = bullish reversal (entry at 3-4pm when daily candle nearing close). — [`myUKta-wicQ` @51:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=myUKta-wicQ&t=3061s)
- VWAP rule for bulls: do not buy below VWAP; buy dips that stay above it. (For trend-following; not for contrarian reversals.) — [`qRirYfM4XiY` @2:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qRirYfM4XiY&t=132s)

### bull flag (6)

- Entry setup: buy first pullback; bull flags with MACD positive. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @35:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2132s)
- Bull flag pattern is primary setup: sharp up move, pullback consolidation, break above consolidation high = entry. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @36:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2160s)
- Bull flag pattern entry: buy after first pullback; stop at low of pullback; profit target at break of high. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @9:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=543s)
- Entry on break of consolidation high of bull flag pattern. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @12:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=735s)
- Prefer entry on the first or second bull flag of the day, typically within the first hour. Flags later in the day are less powerful. — [`VNsh-8cS2Tg` @6:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VNsh-8cS2Tg&t=374s)
- Set stop-loss on ABCD at low of latest dip; entry distance to stop is larger than bull flag but payoff is greater. — [`_4Q0hlNFn-A` @41:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4Q0hlNFn-A&t=2477s)

### hotkeys (14)

- Hotkey offsets: buy on ask with +10 cent offset; sell on bid with -10 cent offset to avoid getting stuck on orders. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @11:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=717s)
- Use hot keys for rapid execution; this setup requires sub-second order entry and exit. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @53:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=3190s)
- Press hotkey to instantly enter position; Ctrl+Z instantly exits full position. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @1:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=62s)
- Start with only three basic hotkeys (buy, sell, cancel orders) before adding advanced ones. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @1:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=108s)
- Hot key buy order: Shift + number (1-10) = buy 1,000-10,000 shares at ask plus 15 cents. — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @13:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=787s)
- Hotkey setup: Shift+1 for buy ask, Ctrl+Z for sell bid, Ctrl+Q to cancel all orders. — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @31:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=1862s)
- Position building: use hotkey (Shift 2) to buy 2,000 shares, then add incrementally; avoid massive early position. — [`gA8r-ZoN49Q` @5:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gA8r-ZoN49Q&t=322s)
- Hotkey setup: Shift+1 to buy, Control+Z to sell full position on the bid. — [`oKlhUSSHe2Q` @60:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=oKlhUSSHe2Q&t=3620s)
- Use hotkey Shift+1 to buy shares at ask+15c offset; Shift+2 for larger position; Control+Q to cancel all orders. — [`p1W8Mjl4kWs` @50:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=p1W8Mjl4kWs&t=3052s)
- Use hotkeys to pre-stage buy orders at key prices; click hotkey to execute instantly when price reaches that level. — [`pJuG5YtVF84` @19:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pJuG5YtVF84&t=1190s)
- Press Shift+1 hotkey to enter an order using all available cash (no leverage) with limit order 20 cents above current price. — [`q_ZliMvD8yc` @4:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q_ZliMvD8yc&t=281s)
- Use hotkeys (e.g., Shift+1 to buy 1000 shares, Ctrl+Z to sell full position) for speed advantage. — [`taHYs-v3hBQ` @8:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=taHYs-v3hBQ&t=521s)
- Program hot keys: Shift for buy orders (1-10 for share sizes), Ctrl for sell orders (Z for full position, X for half, C for quarter), Escape to cancel all orders. — [`w97KlUrVDk0` @21:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w97KlUrVDk0&t=1312s)
- Create hotkey to buy 95% of buying power in each trade (custom hotkey via order script wizard). — [`xTPcI7HHu5w` @12:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xTPcI7HHu5w&t=721s)

### MACD (7)

- When MACD is against the trade, do not buy dips; risk is too high. — [`1FKu4LH0Xss` @28:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1FKu4LH0Xss&t=1692s)
- Buy dips on first pullback after breakout; avoid trading the back-side (after MACD closes). — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @51:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=3068s)
- Buy the pullback during an uptrend; this is the front-side strategy. Do not trade the back-side (after MACD crosses negative). — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @20:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1237s)
- MACD positive = green light to trade long; MACD negative = red light, do not trade long. — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @2:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=128s)
- If MACD has crossed into negative territory, do not take entry to the long side — this is a red light regardless of bullish chart pattern. — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @3:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=192s)
- Break of VWAP setup: stock curls back up through VWAP after pullback with positive MACD; buy the break for continuation higher. — [`P5Sn_mWJdy0` @9:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P5Sn_mWJdy0&t=578s)
- MACD on one-minute chart signals entry opportunity; ignore trades when it turns negative. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @50:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=3038s)

### VWAP (7)

- Rarely short (buy) below VWAP; focus on trading above VWAP for beginners. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @43:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2607s)
- [00:07:32-00:08:15] Entry variation: break of VWAP after daily consolidation period — [`Hxjla5IZYbA`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Hxjla5IZYbA)
- Trade the break above VWAP after stock pulls back and holds it. — [`Uft2BuJpap8` @2:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Uft2BuJpap8&t=171s)
- Stock should fail previous attempts to break over VWAP (creating supply area). [00:05:34] — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @1:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=106s)
- Entry: Look for break back above VWAP on increased buyer demand. — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @6:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=376s)
- When stock reclaims VWAP and breaks above, expect additional leg higher; this is high-probability entry. — [`l7M03OXCIdA` @20:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=l7M03OXCIdA&t=1206s)
- VWAP trap entry: when stock drops below VWAP then bounces back above, can trap short sellers and create squeeze. — [`nm-rhysU96k` @5:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nm-rhysU96k&t=340s)

### stop at pullback low (8)

- Stop-loss should be placed at the low of the pullback (proper entry at $6, stop at $5.90). — [`8eLtork_M50` @10:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8eLtork_M50&t=604s)
- First entry: break above high of pullback candle; stop placed at low of pullback candle. — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @40:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=2415s)
- Stop loss: the low of the red candle (the candle before entry). Example: if red candle low is 1606, stop at 1605. — [`VNsh-8cS2Tg` @4:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VNsh-8cS2Tg&t=257s)
- Entry: over the high of the flagpole (first 5-minute breakout). Stop: low of pullback. — [`Xdw5azEqs6o` @1:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xdw5azEqs6o&t=97s)
- Risk should be on initial entry (tight stop); subsequent pullbacks carry less risk. — [`Xdw5azEqs6o`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xdw5azEqs6o)
- Stop loss: place at low of last pullback (the last wave down). Tighter stops on pullback entries than on extended entries. — [`iBNcF_lPBIg` @32:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iBNcF_lPBIg&t=1925s)
- Stop is set below the pullback low, typically 50 cents to a dollar away from entry. — [`uoig-Z6qtV0` @13:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uoig-Z6qtV0&t=788s)
- Stop-loss placement: place stops at low of pullback to create good risk-to-reward ratio on entry. — [`vPMyjSCgf58` @33:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vPMyjSCgf58&t=2007s)

### whole/half dollar level (5)

- Buy the dip on one-minute pullbacks when stock is squeezing up; target the next whole dollar or half-dollar level for exit. — [`6w-7geIsq1A` @8:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6w-7geIsq1A&t=504s)
- Best trade entry: break of the half dollar (RGSC at $4.50 with 6200 shares). — [`8eLtork_M50` @2:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8eLtork_M50&t=163s)
- Enter dips near half dollar or whole dollar psychological levels (e.g., $4.00, $4.50). — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @2:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=145s)
- Rule 2: Entries near half dollars and whole dollars. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @31:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=1868s)
- Entry around whole dollars/half dollars: expect consolidation then breakout through level. — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @42:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=2570s)

### max loss per trade (5)

- Define max loss on every trade before entry; knowing your max loss is mandatory before any trade executes. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @24:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=1441s)
- Risk definition: Risk is the difference between entry price and max loss price (e.g., $8 entry minus $7.90 stop = $0.10 per share risk). — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @8:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=510s)
- Risk management: calculate maximum loss per trade (entry price minus stop price × shares); maximum daily loss is $5,000; must have favorable risk-to-reward ratio (ideally 1:2.5 or better). — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @40:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=2416s)
- Set max loss trigger at 20% giveback of daily profit; if losing more, stop trading for day. — [`qPb8LF1k8vU` @12:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qPb8LF1k8vU&t=734s)
- Half-dollar and whole-dollar setup: buy the break; max loss just under the level. — [`y-sFO1ouTX4` @40:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y-sFO1ouTX4&t=2457s)

### level 2 / time and sales (7)

- Entry timing: Wait for green orders on tape (buy signal) to confirm sentiment shift before entering on pullback. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @10:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=613s)
- Large buy order on Level 2 (e.g., 200,000 shares) = confirmation to size up and be more aggressive. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @14:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=870s)
- Watch for "bursts of green" in time and sales (transactions executed at the offer) = buy signal. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @39:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=2372s)
- Level 2 critical level test: if stock can't break above a critical level and sellers keep reloading, exit and avoid (don't get "smoked"). — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @1:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=114s)
- Confirm entry with level 2: watch green-on-tape (buying pressure) BEFORE committing full position. Do not anticipate; wait for confirmation. — [`iBNcF_lPBIg` @25:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iBNcF_lPBIg&t=1548s)
- When green appears on the tape after entry, expect price to move higher; if red or white (in-between spread) appears instead, exit the trade as momentum is weak. — [`q5DRctM5C-Q` @38:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q5DRctM5C-Q&t=2280s)
- Enter on the first sign of green on the tape after the initial drop from the IPO open. — [`uoig-Z6qtV0` @11:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uoig-Z6qtV0&t=660s)

### emotional control (7)

- Trade one entry, one exit per day on small cash account; discipline prevents spiral losses. — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @53:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=3183s)
- If you lose money on a stock and watch it rip without you, the next entry should be a pullback entry, not a revenge chase. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @7:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=432s)
- Do not buy middle of candle during FOMO; wait for first red candle (pullback) to form, then green candle back to highs. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @34:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=2086s)
- Sell winners too soon and hold losers too long is the fear-based trap. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @40:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=2427s)
- Emotional hedge theory: trade opposite side based on psychology; if going long dip, think "where would short sellers have stop loss?" (high of day); they cover = up. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @18:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1090s)
- First trade loss ($271): still small, no emotional trigger. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @9:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=544s)
- STEP 5: Exit immediately if stock does not move higher upon entry; tighter exit discipline required. — [`trcKOZnoPPs` @15:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=trcKOZnoPPs&t=925s)

### halts (5)

- Halt resumption dip-and-rip: wait for halt resumption, buy on dip, sell on rip higher. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @2:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=159s)
- Dip-and-rip: after halt resumption, buy the dip; when price rips back up, scale out. — [`jpPgEAAVMxY` @8:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jpPgEAAVMxY&t=480s)
- Halt and resume pattern: when stock halts up, opens, then dips and rips back up, this creates momentum entry opportunity. — [`lkNRTWLroqY` @14:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=lkNRTWLroqY&t=842s)
- Halt and resumption strategy: use halt openings to enter positions; gap up on open then look for dip and rip. — [`nm-rhysU96k` @5:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nm-rhysU96k&t=328s)
- Dip and rip timing: enter on resumption after halt down, betting on bounce back above previous levels. — [`nm-rhysU96k` @5:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nm-rhysU96k&t=352s)

### high of day break (2)

- [00:04:09-00:04:31] Must not over-leverage into high of day break; good entry with stop means profit is captured even if breakout fails — [`IHeJROuEzRU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IHeJROuEzRU)
- Short-seller stop-loss theory: short sellers short off high of day with stop at high of day; weak breakout = they hold/add; strong breakout = they cover (buy). — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @4:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=273s)

### scaling out (2)

- Scale out strategy: sell half on first dip if trade loses 5 cents after entry; keeps losses small if trade continues down. — [`1axXKjgCe84` @14:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1axXKjgCe84&t=860s)
- When trading IRA account: instead of swinging for home runs, take profits at regular intervals and move gains into long-term mutual funds. — [`DoO4YnbPOPA` @43:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoO4YnbPOPA&t=2591s)

### break-even stop (3)

- Entry confirmation: Price must move up at least 10 cents within first minute of entry to set stop at break-even. [00:49:01] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @49:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=2941s)
- Stop at break-even rule: Move stop loss to entry price once up 10 cents to eliminate risk. [00:49:01] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @53:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3225s)
- After entry, move stop to breakeven once price moves above entry. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @9:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=540s)

### A-quality setup only (2)

- Do not force a trade when you miss an entry; wait for the next quality setup. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @6:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=400s)
- Entry quality rule: only trade quality setups; avoid B-grade and C-grade setups in cold markets. — [`vPMyjSCgf58` @18:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vPMyjSCgf58&t=1088s)

### profit-loss ratio 2:1 (2)

- Risk is the difference between entry price and exit price, not the total shares purchased. For a $3 stock: buy 1,000 shares ($3,000 buying power) with 5% stop loss ($150 risk) and 10% profit target ($300 profit) for a 2:1 ratio. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @6:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=365s)
- Set stop-loss orders immediately upon entry; use tight 10-cent stops to condition yourself to small losses. [00:22:16] — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @22:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=1322s)

### uncategorised (97)

- Wait for stocks below $1 to break above $1 per share before aggressively trading; decimal places thin out and trading accelerates at $1+. — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @12:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=753s)
- Wait for pullback before entry on breakout setups; do not chase at highs. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @27:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=1627s)
- Do not overcompensate for a missed entry by getting in too high. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @9:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=540s)
- Buy the first and second pullback within a trend. Expect the third pullback to be a false breakout; stop trading there. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @27:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1623s)
- Use stair-stepping: buy pullback, ride leg up, repeat. Continue adding into strength. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @30:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1801s)
- Candle over candle (green candle breaking high of previous candle) is a bullish signal; buy when it breaks that high. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @19:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=1144s)
- Enter the moment a candle breaks the high of the previous candle—be faster than technical indicator traders. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @19:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=1155s)
- Momentum trading approach: "Buy high, sell higher"; buy stocks already moving, not stationary ones. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @4:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=299s)
- Using leverage (6x on $1,000 account = $6,000 buying power); risk calculation is still entry-to-stop, not total position size. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @13:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=818s)
- Accuracy is primary metric; avoid holding losers too long or doubling down. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @19:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=1158s)
- [00:08:14-00:08:35] Safer entry: wait for initial breakout, let stock come back down for retest of level, buy dip with stop underneath psychological level — [`4jes2_N3m2I`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4jes2_N3m2I)
- When stock is moving, buy at the ask rather than waiting on the bid; avoid missing the opportunity to save one penny per share. — [`4syXgXshgsc` @6:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=406s)
- Weeble simulator execution: Took ~9 seconds buy, ~4 seconds sell (slower than production account). — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @15:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=942s)
- Thinkorswim order lag issue: Buy orders can sit in working order tab for 15-30+ seconds before executing. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @20:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=1238s)
- Bias: long side (not short) for these bottoming bounces. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @4:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=287s)
- Aggressive entry: breaking through high-of-day; arbitrary stop: 15-20 cents if rejected. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @6:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=381s)
- Enter on the first pullback after stock squeezes up, looking for change in trend (doji, hammer, or small red candle followed by green). — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @30:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=1808s)
- Offset buy orders 10 cents ABOVE the ask to ensure fill despite market movement and slippage. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @3:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=187s)
- Get entry confirmation on daily chart before zooming into 5-min and 1-min charts. — [`BFH-0N8S-IA` @6:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BFH-0N8S-IA&t=390s)
- Buy first and second pullback on one-minute chart; do not trade third pullback (too extended). — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @52:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=3137s)
- Use "buy ask" order type for volatile stocks to avoid slippage vs. market orders. — [`C4XZ_67c0Fo` @6:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=C4XZ_67c0Fo&t=369s)
- Enter only when stock price is between $2 and $20, the price range with most momentum. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @2:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=135s)
- Dip trade entry: Only after price confirmed above prior consolidation high. — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @15:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=904s)
- Entry timing: wait for stock to make a move, pull back, then execute entry—don't chase initial pop. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @34:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=2074s)
- 5-minute chart pullback + 1-minute candle breakout over high = multi-timeframe alignment for strong entries. — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @3:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=187s)
- Focus on momentum trading: buy stocks moving up, sell dips higher (buy high, sell higher). — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @10:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=620s)
- Do not buy stocks going down hoping they turn around. — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @6:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=400s)
- Keep all cash until seeing a stock that is moving; then enter with full buying power. — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @9:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=560s)
- Use 10-second chart and 1-minute chart for entry timing on fast-moving stocks. — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @21:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=1318s)
- Use "breakout or bailout" philosophy: if stock stalls after entry, exit instead of holding and hoping. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @6:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=401s)

## stop-loss (154 rules)

### daily loss limit (36)

- Guard rails: stop trading if hit max daily loss; three big losses in excess of amount = stop; abandon if fails to keep going up. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @35:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2143s)
- Stop trading aggressively once MACD crosses below the signal line; the window has closed. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @33:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1982s)
- Daily max loss equals daily goal (e.g., if daily goal is $5,000, max loss is $5,000). — [`Bqo2XGDZtVU` @42:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Bqo2XGDZtVU&t=2566s)
- Once max loss is hit on a day, stop trading and do not continue adding risk. — [`Bqo2XGDZtVU` @43:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Bqo2XGDZtVU&t=2611s)
- Trade only first two hours of day (9:30-11:30 a.m. ET); stop trading afternoon to preserve decision-making quality. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @28:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=1704s)
- RULE 1: Give back half your gain = time to walk away. Stop trading if you've lost 50% of unrealized profit. — [`H9BRF4xynQA` @8:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=H9BRF4xynQA&t=536s)
- Set a daily max loss rule and follow it consistently; this is a critical guard rail for beginners. [00:09:50] — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @8:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=526s)
- Green-to-red rule: if you turn a green day red, stop trading immediately. — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @10:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=605s)
- If you give back half of your daily goal after hitting it, stop and walk away. — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @10:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=616s)
- Three strikes rule: Three consecutive losses = stop trading for the day (author sometimes breaks this). [00:33:05] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @33:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=1985s)
- MACD negative crossing = stop trading that stock; take foot off the gas. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @35:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=2151s)
- Set maximum daily loss equal to daily profit goal; stop trading for the day once this limit is hit. — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @20:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=1209s)
- If up more than half daily goal, give back more than half profit on the day, stop and walk away with remainder. — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @20:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=1251s)
- Maintain a maximum daily loss threshold; once hit, stop trading for the day. — [`RLGDwe2m70E` @50:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RLGDwe2m70E&t=3034s)
- Stop trading a stock once the MACD crosses negative; the trend has ended. — [`SLMXOd13uqI` @51:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SLMXOd13uqI&t=3066s)
- Walk away when you give back ~10% of gains instead of holding until max loss. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @39:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2355s)
- Daily stop-loss: Walk away if you hit your maximum daily loss (which equals your average daily gain). — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @39:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=2340s)
- Stop watching market action once you've decided to walk away for the day. — [`anuxv488waA` @10:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=anuxv488waA&t=618s)
- Daily max loss: Set equal to daily profit target (e.g., $200 goal = $200 max loss). [00:52:35] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @53:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3181s)
- Three strikes rule: Three consecutive losing trades = stop trading for the day. [00:33:05] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @54:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3255s)
- Three strikes you're out: if three losses in a row, stop trading for the day. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @12:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=721s)
- Stop trading after 11:00 a.m. if you want to optimize results (metrics-driven rule). — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @17:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=1021s)
- Build daily cushion with first 3-4 trades; if up $300-400, set daily loss stop at $100 to avoid turning green days red. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @20:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=1206s)
- Stop trading if market window closes without opportunity (no point forcing trades late day). — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @16:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=962s)
- If you have not taken a trade in 30 minutes, stop trading for the day. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @66:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=3960s)
- Implement a daily shut-off valve; stop trading once you hit a specific loss limit (example: down $500, stop for the day). — [`mbIwWiLY4DQ` @9:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mbIwWiLY4DQ&t=572s)
- Exit rule: When MACD line crosses below signal line, stop trading that move; look for next crossover [00:11:15] — [`mfGQr2tHoX0` @11:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mfGQr2tHoX0&t=672s)
- Max daily loss rule: $5,000 maximum loss threshold; statistical data shows 80% chance of doubling loss if trading continues past max loss. — [`nxelwVBl0iA` @7:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nxelwVBl0iA&t=437s)
- Set max daily loss stop at $5,000 on red days; stop trading in hot market only if down more than $5,000. — [`qPb8LF1k8vU` @13:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qPb8LF1k8vU&t=799s)
- Daily profit management: walk away if profit retraces more than 50%; stop trading if giving back 25% or more from peak. — [`qlhkAdaOg_Q` @39:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qlhkAdaOg_Q&t=2392s)
- _...6 more._

### stop at pullback low (35)

- Stop loss set at the low of the pullback candle. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @37:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=2226s) _(2 videos)_
- Stop is at the low of the pullback candle. — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @14:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=886s)
- Stop loss placement: at the low of the pullback bottoming tail. — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @26:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=1606s)
- Stop-loss: low of pullback, never wider. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @1:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=87s)
- Stop strategy: place stops at the low of the pullback; if pullback is too large, position is too risky. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @30:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1822s)
- Stop loss placement at the low of the pullback candle(s), which allows tighter risk management compared to stopping at the low of the entire initial move. — [`HYoQYCBW4sw` @39:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HYoQYCBW4sw&t=2341s)
- Stop loss = low of the pullback candle. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @48:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=2919s)
- Stop at the low of the pullback candles. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @22:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1353s)
- Stop: low of pullback candle (15 cents in whiteboard example). — [`UNoPqBWuLC0` @3:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UNoPqBWuLC0&t=182s)

- Stop-loss placement: Stop-loss at the low of the pullback, not wider. — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @11:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=692s)
- Stop loss placed at the low of pullback; profit target at retest to high of day. — [`YiMItz4fgFk` @34:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YiMItz4fgFk&t=2049s)
- Stop goes at the low of the previous candle (the pullback), never wider. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @4:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=242s)
- Stop loss is placed at the low of the pullback (bull flag pattern). — [`_0wygrb5pPs` @51:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_0wygrb5pPs&t=3062s)
- Set stop-loss at the low of the pullback candle in a bull flag setup, not wider. — [`_4Q0hlNFn-A` @27:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4Q0hlNFn-A&t=1654s)
- Set stop-loss at the low of the pullback, never wider. — [`_fsRWw0VbS4` @19:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_fsRWw0VbS4&t=1178s)
- Stop placement: low of pullback candle after breakout. — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @50:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=3005s)
- Stop loss at low of pullback candle on 5-minute chart. — [`ag5JoiOlUy4` @1:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ag5JoiOlUy4&t=97s)
- Stop loss must be at low of pullback, never wider. — [`bvy1pyzTrG4`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bvy1pyzTrG4)
- Stop loss: place at the low of the previous pullback candle. — [`dvetF0H3pNo` @34:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dvetF0H3pNo&t=2062s)
