- Stop loss at the low of the pullback; profit target at previous high of day. — [`js25lIZMUSY` @42:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=js25lIZMUSY&t=2521s)
- Stop loss placement: the low of the pullback, never wider. This defines your maximum risk. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @16:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=1016s)
- Stop loss: place at the low of the pullback (about $0.50 per share in the example shown). — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @36:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=2203s)
- Stop-loss at the low of the pullback candle, never wider. — [`oKlhUSSHe2Q` @46:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=oKlhUSSHe2Q&t=2779s)
- Use arbitrary 10-15 cent stop loss (not at low of pullback, which is too far). — [`p1W8Mjl4kWs` @34:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=p1W8Mjl4kWs&t=2050s)
- Stop loss placed at the low of the pullback wave, never wider. — [`pYx756KSvR4` @28:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pYx756KSvR4&t=1722s)
- Stop loss should be at the low of the dip on a 10-second chart (e.g., 555). — [`q_ZliMvD8yc` @5:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q_ZliMvD8yc&t=318s)
- Set stop at the low of the pullback, never wider. — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @29:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=1775s)
- _...5 more._

### max loss per trade (19)

- If you break a Max Loss rule, you have an 80% likelihood of doubling your loss if you continue trading. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @8:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=498s)
- Implement hard $1,000 max loss rule: cannot initiate new trades once down $1,000 for the day. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @43:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2612s)
- Set stop order just below support level as max loss exit. — [`ICbPz3iXbG0` @20:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ICbPz3iXbG0&t=1221s)
- On pullback, place max loss at the low of the pullback; target 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @1:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=103s)
- Max loss per trade: Not stated in this session, but referenced from prior framework. Author took trade that "didn't have any of those except the flow was kind of on the low side." [00:03:34] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @4:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=274s)
- No strict max loss rule historically worked well; recovery from down days possible later same day. — [`M07xElrhVYM` @50:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=3057s)
- Max loss per trade: use 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio minimum; only need 33% accuracy to break even at this ratio. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @13:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=818s)
- Risk no more than 2% of account per trade maximum. On $1,000 account, risk is capped at $20 per trade. — [`RoAVk--vljU` @6:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RoAVk--vljU&t=391s)
- Max loss is at the low of the control candle; profit target is 2:1 risk-to-reward minimum. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @51:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=3072s)
- Max loss per trade: Set based on willingness to risk; example: $50 risk = need $100 profit target. [00:37:00] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @52:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3123s)
- Walk-away triggers: (1) Hit max loss, (2) Give back 50% of daily gains, (3) 20% drawdown from highs, (4) Feel angry/emotionally activated. [00:52:30] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @54:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3290s)
- Successful red day = losing money while following rules and respecting risk parameters (no revenge trading, no exceeding max loss). — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @1:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=62s)
- Practical max loss should be smaller than stated max loss (e.g., $1,000-$1,500 vs $5,000 stated). — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @15:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=916s)
- First month: risk $50 per trade; target $125 daily profit. If you hit max loss, close trading for the day. — [`mbIwWiLY4DQ` @10:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mbIwWiLY4DQ&t=604s)
- Calculate new risk limit: set hard line at 2x max loss ($10,000); remaining capital can be risked on recovery trades. — [`nxelwVBl0iA` @7:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nxelwVBl0iA&t=470s)
- Max loss rule: if max loss stop is hit, exit immediately. — [`p1W8Mjl4kWs` @50:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=p1W8Mjl4kWs&t=3039s)
- Max loss rule: 10% of account per day. Do not trade if max loss is hit (prevents emotional decisions). — [`q_ZliMvD8yc` @9:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q_ZliMvD8yc&t=566s)
- Max loss per trade example: 25,000 shares × $0.10 stop = $2,500 max loss on first trade. — [`vPMyjSCgf58` @14:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vPMyjSCgf58&t=857s)
- Know your max loss and profit target before entering a trade. — [`zPJ4oemntmU` @8:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=zPJ4oemntmU&t=487s)

### support and resistance (12)

- [00:10:44-00:10:48] Stop placement just underneath recent support level — [`D8Guwf84eAA`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=D8Guwf84eAA)
- Proper exit on reversal: Stop always at high of day for shorts, first target is 9 EMA support. [00:06:08] — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @6:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=374s)
- Set stop loss at psychological support level; if support breaks, exit position. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @4:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=242s)
- [00:03:51-00:04:09] Stop placement: below the double bottom at point C, use moving average support — [`IHeJROuEzRU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IHeJROuEzRU)
- Profit target is retest of previous high; stop loss at support holding post-breakout. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @21:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=1311s)
- Set stop below the ascending support trendline formed during pull-back action. — [`MTbYDUswdbM` @1:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MTbYDUswdbM&t=108s)
- Stop loss management: Trail stop up as position moves higher; move stop just above previous resistance level. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @22:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1370s)
- Stop loss when breakout fails; if stock cannot break through expected resistance, exit rather than hold. — [`RUuJzmz5jCg` @6:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RUuJzmz5jCg&t=419s)
- Support definition: Low of last pullback; use this level as stop-loss placement. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @10:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=602s)
- Stop placement: Set stop just below support level (e.g., if bought at $6.00, stop at $5.80 = 20 cent stop). — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @21:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1300s)
- Set stop above resistance levels; false breakout wicks indicate weak pattern. — [`c5HU_4ukSw8` @9:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=c5HU_4ukSw8&t=553s)
- Set stops based on chart support/resistance, not on spread size; a 2-point technical stop is preferable to a 50-cent spread stop. — [`pJuG5YtVF84` @22:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pJuG5YtVF84&t=1371s)

### break-even stop (10)

- Set hard stops at 8-10 cents on small size, or set stop at break-even once up 5-10 cents. — [`1axXKjgCe84` @13:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1axXKjgCe84&t=812s)
- Scale out: sell half at first profit, adjust stop to breakeven on remaining. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @40:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=2445s)
- When selling winners, sell half the position at first profit target (typically around 20 cents profit) and hold other half with stop at breakeven. — [`DoO4YnbPOPA` @42:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoO4YnbPOPA&t=2521s)
- Scale into winners: when up more than average winner (15 cents), double position and move stop to break-even. [00:24:23] — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @23:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=1408s)
- When scaling out, take half profits at first target, then set remaining position's stop at breakeven. — [`V9za4wt9ONo` @16:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V9za4wt9ONo&t=990s)
- After first breakout, move stop to break-even or slightly above low of flag. — [`Xdw5azEqs6o`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xdw5azEqs6o)
- Set profit stop at breakeven to protect position once in profit zone; tighten stop below moving average once higher. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @11:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=673s)
- When in a winning trade, double your position; set stop at break-even after doubling. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @74:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=4466s)
- Scaling out: sell half at first target, adjust stop to breakeven on remainder. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @28:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=1731s)
- When scaling into winners: double position size; move stop to break-even as protection. — [`y-sFO1ouTX4` @52:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y-sFO1ouTX4&t=3162s)

### scaling out (3)

- In colder market, sell half when up 10%, hold rest with trailing stop. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @29:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1772s)
- Adjust stop after profit: if green candle breaks the low of the previous green candle, sell. Tight stop allows scaling out as profit grows. — [`VNsh-8cS2Tg` @5:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VNsh-8cS2Tg&t=326s)
- Take profit at least half on first target; hold rest with trailing stop. — [`Xdw5azEqs6o`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xdw5azEqs6o)

### profit-loss ratio 2:1 (4)

- Risk per share: 6 cents; profit target: 12 cents (2:1 golden ratio minimum). — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @4:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=248s)
- Profit target calculation: if stop is 20 cents, need 40 cent upside (2:1 ratio). — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @40:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=2438s)
- Risk per trade: if risking $100, position max is fixed until 2:1 upside available. — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @42:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=2567s)
- Profit target: Retest of the high of day using 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio (if risk is 20 cents, target is 40 cents up). — [`hLn6LrlXgAE` @20:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLn6LrlXgAE&t=1223s)

### emotional control (3)

- Stop immediately after one or two high-pressure losses; do not attempt revenge trading. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @31:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=1918s)
- Post-exit discipline: Do not return to trading after walking away; stop watching for the day. — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @39:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=2340s)
- Smaller position size makes it emotionally easier to cut losses. — [`mBT72-bFlPM` @14:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mBT72-bFlPM&t=883s)

### trailing stop (3)

- Adjust new stop to trailing minus 5% stop. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @3:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=180s)
- Use trailing stop (5%) that moves with the stock as it rises. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @30:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1845s)
- Position management: If trade moves higher, consider adding to position; trail stop up as price rises. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @22:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1321s)

### adding to a winner (2)

- Add to positions into breaks of whole-dollar levels; move stop-loss up as position accumulates. — [`SLMXOd13uqI` @40:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SLMXOd13uqI&t=2403s)
- Never add to losing positions; cut losses ruthlessly and add to winning positions instead. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @74:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=4461s)

### reversal (2)

- Reversal strategy: short the first candle making new low after consecutive green candles, stop at the high. — [`eCSzHYl8apo` @9:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eCSzHYl8apo&t=598s)
- Hold short position with stop at high of move or wait for reversal candle. — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @13:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=813s)

### halts (2)

- In cold market, don't short because unlimited risk is not worth it; one gap-up halt can wipe out months of profits. — [`HY7i78bOJFA` @6:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HY7i78bOJFA&t=385s)
- Do not trade halts down—check halt levels before taking dip trade; halt down level must be further away than your stop to avoid trap. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @16:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=961s)

### uncategorised (23)

- Stop using technical indicators and learn to read candlesticks directly from the raw data. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @0:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=10s)
- Use tight stops and cut losses quickly. Control key: press Ctrl+Z to exit immediately. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @38:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=2321s)
- If the first trade fails (even with tight stop), recognize immediately that market is cold and reduce overall activity for the day. — [`5AV2OLWD1gc` @15:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5AV2OLWD1gc&t=951s)
- When level-2 shows large seller wall (e.g., 18,000 shares at $7.10, 30,000 at $7.20), anticipate potential false breakout after short-stop squeeze. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @15:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=900s)
- Stop at 9:30 a.m. — Hard stop from previous day's collapse that occurred at market open. — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @2:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=148s)
- Stop if you have three consecutive losses of $200 each or bigger (about $500 down). — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @20:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=1224s)
- When trading a stock with a 50-cent spread at the ask, your stop must realistically be at least the width of the spread below the bid, not a tighter stop. — [`OLvNqb3-xnY` @6:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLvNqb3-xnY&t=376s)
- Stop checking windows: actively trade between 7:00 a.m. and 11:00 a.m. Eastern based on 24,446 trades analyzed. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @31:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1914s)
- Stop loss: if the stock breaks back below VWAP, that signals the setup has failed. — [`SCy6RrASpJY` @7:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SCy6RrASpJY&t=467s)
- Enforce strict stop-loss orders; scalp traders must manage losses carefully because they use large position sizes. — [`SLMXOd13uqI` @7:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SLMXOd13uqI&t=422s)
- Add on breakouts every 10 cents per share as stock pushes higher; adjust stop each time. — [`SLMXOd13uqI` @47:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SLMXOd13uqI&t=2824s)
- Use daily goal as reference point, not hard stop; dig deeper on hot market days. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @38:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2295s)
- When market is working: continue trading longer; when market is cold: stop earlier. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @39:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2341s)
- Set a stop loss immediately when shorting; losses can be theoretically unlimited. — [`TTvaXCmtVbI` @8:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TTvaXCmtVbI&t=510s)
- Cut losses immediately rather than holding and hoping. — [`V9za4wt9ONo` @14:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V9za4wt9ONo&t=841s)
- Hard stop at noon: no trading after 12:00 p.m. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @18:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=1088s)
- Walking away at noon hard stop is non-negotiable for this trader (based on metrics). — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @12:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=723s)
- Take short on break of shooting star low; stop at high of shooting star candle. — [`kQ93wnMCMWQ` @3:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kQ93wnMCMWQ&t=184s)
- If you give back your cushion (profit), reduce back to quarter size or stop entirely. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @66:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=3975s)
- Preferred price range for scalping: $1 to $20 per stock (under $1 has low risk but high spreads; over $20 has too much risk per tick). — [`p1W8Mjl4kWs` @14:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=p1W8Mjl4kWs&t=874s)
- Stop out of false breakouts quickly rather than averaging down or holding for bounces. — [`vOZGdT9nYbc` @16:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vOZGdT9nYbc&t=984s)
- Profit-taking: exit with 10-15% giveback from daily peak (hard stop at 50% giveback). — [`vPMyjSCgf58` @36:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vPMyjSCgf58&t=2208s)
- Profit calculation principle: if 70% accuracy with consistent $100-$200 risk per trade, use those fixed amounts consistently. — [`yFKCn5e1ERc` @7:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yFKCn5e1ERc&t=442s)

## exit (146 rules)

### scaling out (25)

- Scale out partial position into moves higher, capture gains without giving back too much. — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @14:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=895s)
- Exit discipline: take profit and walk away; do not overstay once choppy price action begins. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @21:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=1298s)
- Create multiple sell hotkeys: sell full position, sell half, sell quarter, sell at midpoint, sell at ask. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @9:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=546s)
- Modify hotkey: change sell order from "sell full position" to "sell half position" to force discipline in holding winners. — [`Ai6r6xjk8Uk` @24:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ai6r6xjk8Uk&t=1467s)
- Hold trailing portion of position (after selling half) until Heikin-Ashi reversal signals, then exit. — [`Ai6r6xjk8Uk` @26:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ai6r6xjk8Uk&t=1570s)
- Use sell half hotkey (Ctrl+X) instead of sell full position to force discipline in profit-taking. — [`C4XZ_67c0Fo` @11:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=C4XZ_67c0Fo&t=706s)
- Scale out of position as stock pulls back; do not overstay trade when momentum slows or big buyers disappear. — [`DAnpW98ZCSg` @10:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DAnpW98ZCSg&t=642s)
- After selling half at 20 cents, sell another quarter when up another 20 cents, continue halving position as profit continues. — [`DoO4YnbPOPA` @42:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoO4YnbPOPA&t=2523s)
- Take partial profits at resistance: sell half on first test of whole dollar level ($11.00). — [`IKwm-KdCqpg` @3:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IKwm-KdCqpg&t=215s)
- Sell half once up 10%. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @3:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=180s)
- If support holds, look for bounce-back spring move; can then add to position or take profit. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @21:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1286s)
- Focus on scaling out of winners instead of selling the entire position at once. — [`V9za4wt9ONo` @15:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V9za4wt9ONo&t=901s)
- Secondary exit: Sell half the position as profit is being made to pay yourself before profit potentially disappears. — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @49:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=2950s)
- Exit strategy: Take profits and call it a day when targets are met [00:20:06] — [`ZRRCyIoZnHE` @20:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZRRCyIoZnHE&t=1225s)
- Position management: scale out into strength, don't hold full position to absolute top. — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @41:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=2502s)
- Scaling strategy: hold core position; scale out on first candle-under-candle pattern for profit taking. — [`dvetF0H3pNo` @49:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dvetF0H3pNo&t=2957s)
- Hold through bulk of move rather than selling too early on ask; selling half and holding rest gives opportunity for bigger profit. — [`eY7JdVjg0kw` @16:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eY7JdVjg0kw&t=960s)
- Exit: when big seller appears at resistance and stock stalls; take profit rather than hold through seller. — [`jpPgEAAVMxY` @4:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jpPgEAAVMxY&t=240s)
- Take profit when you have 15-20 cents per share gain, unless trade is moving very fast. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @74:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=4476s)
- Do not overstay; take profit and exit rather than holding for maximum gain. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @47:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=2876s)
- Phase five: learn to scale out of winners. — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @38:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=2300s)
- When holding into a halt, estimate risk and decide whether to hold or scale out before liquidity dries up. — [`vOZGdT9nYbc` @10:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vOZGdT9nYbc&t=638s)
- Scale out on micro pullbacks or after partial gains rather than holding entire position. — [`vOZGdT9nYbc` @12:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vOZGdT9nYbc&t=773s)
- Hold winners; take profit on dips only after a new high is confirmed. — [`y-sFO1ouTX4` @18:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y-sFO1ouTX4&t=1101s)
- Take profit into consolidation breaks: sold going into halt when stock hit $6.85. — [`yBiAZvwpVww` @7:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yBiAZvwpVww&t=459s)

### level 2 / time and sales (11)

- Cancel trade if Level 2 shows big sellers on the ask or many red (sell) orders on time-and-sales. — [`4syXgXshgsc` @50:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=3032s)
- When observing Level 2: look for situations where large sell volume persists but price doesn't break support. — [`BaZ4R2ovI9k` @6:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BaZ4R2ovI9k&t=406s)
- Holding winners: Stay in position if trend remains strong (green tape); don't sell too early out of fear. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @23:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1391s)
- Before taking a trade with a good candlestick pattern, verify Level 2 does not show large sell orders blocking price movement. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @14:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=842s)
- Large sell order on Level 2 (e.g., 200,000 shares) = resist taking the trade despite good chart setup. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @14:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=860s)
- Exit indicators: large sell orders on Level 2, large topping tails, high-volume red candles, slowing buying on tape (time and sales), price stops moving up. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @83:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=4993s)
- Exit indicators: Sell if price drops 10 cents, volume declines, or big sellers appear on Level 2. [00:50:24] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @50:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3002s)
- Exit indicator: absence of strength. If level 2 shows: big seller appears, burst of red trades (selling), or price stalls = time to exit. — [`iBNcF_lPBIg` @36:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iBNcF_lPBIg&t=2165s)
- Big seller appears on Level 2 = exit indicator. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @44:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=2642s)
- Exit indicators: (1) green candles getting smaller = trend exhaustion; (2) topping tail or doji = reversal imminent; (3) big sellers on level 2 = exit signal. — [`p1W8Mjl4kWs` @50:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=p1W8Mjl4kWs&t=3030s)
- Exit signal: first candle making a new low OR large topping tail OR stock no longer obvious OR large seller on Level 2. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @30:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=1834s)

### support and resistance (6)

- Exit when price action and technicals no longer support trade, regardless of uninvested profit left on table. — [`016ibIK58t0` @16:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=016ibIK58t0&t=967s)
- Exit partially on breakout of psychological whole-dollar resistance levels like $5 a share. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @26:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=1616s)
- At [00:24:06] When price tries to break resistance at $6.20 with big sell orders visible on the heat map, predict the price will reject and pull back down. — [`OQLfHuO8KlI` @24:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OQLfHuO8KlI&t=1443s)
- Partial profit-taking: sell 1/4 position at first resistance, reduce full position by thirds on moves higher. — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @41:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=2476s)
- Use 9-period moving average as first profit target resistance on one-minute chart. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @9:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=556s)
- Target after daily breakout: look for room to the next major resistance level (200 MA in this case around 1127). — [`nM2MxYk6Eo0` @3:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nM2MxYk6Eo0&t=216s)

### profit-loss ratio 2:1 (9)

- On first green month, target 60% accuracy with one trade per day and 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio. — [`RLGDwe2m70E` @36:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RLGDwe2m70E&t=2164s)
- Target: high of day (35 cents in whiteboard example = 2:1 risk-reward). — [`UNoPqBWuLC0` @3:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UNoPqBWuLC0&t=193s)
- [02:32:13-02:32:21] Set profit target and maximum acceptable loss before entering a trade; maintain a minimum 1:1 profit-to-loss ratio (target profit ÷ acceptable loss). — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)
- Minimum profit-to-loss ratio: Target at least a 2:1 ratio to achieve profitability with reasonable accuracy. — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @11:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=692s)
- Risk-reward minimum is 1:1; aim for 2:1 ratio. If risking 10 cents, target 20 cents. — [`_0wygrb5pPs` @60:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_0wygrb5pPs&t=3601s)
- Minimum profit target should provide at least 2:1 risk-to-reward ratio; aim high on initial targets. — [`_4Q0hlNFn-A` @29:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4Q0hlNFn-A&t=1747s)
- Profit target: 2x the risk amount (2:1 profit-to-loss ratio). — [`dvetF0H3pNo` @34:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dvetF0H3pNo&t=2077s)
- Profit/loss ratio target: 2:1 (willing to risk $100 to make $200). — [`iIC62xnblLc` @17:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iIC62xnblLc&t=1079s)
- Apply a risk-reward ratio where winners are bigger than losers; target minimum 2:1 profit-loss ratio. — [`mbIwWiLY4DQ` @9:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mbIwWiLY4DQ&t=547s)

### reversal (6)

- Exit immediately if trade does not confirm as planned; do not hold hoping for reversal. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @3:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=198s)
- "Breakout or bailout": if trade doesn't work immediately, exit early (do not hold waiting for reversal). — [`8eLtork_M50` @24:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8eLtork_M50&t=1454s)
- Weak candle (indecisive, spinning top, doji, shooting star, hanging man): indicates potential reversal at end of rally; avoid or exit. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @9:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=590s)
- When reversal candle forms with dramatic topping tail, exit may not require waiting for candle close. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @31:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1860s)
- Shooting star or gravestone doji at move top = exit signal; anticipate reversal. — [`dvetF0H3pNo` @35:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dvetF0H3pNo&t=2158s)
- Volume bars (up and down) confirm candlestick signals. High volume on reversal candle = "volume top" (sell signal). — [`qRirYfM4XiY` @6:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qRirYfM4XiY&t=415s)

### MACD (8)

- Exit indicators: high-volume red candle, large topping tail, stock no longer obvious, or MACD crossing into negative. — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @33:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=1981s)
- MACD indicator role: open MACD signals uptrend intact; MACD crossing over signals exit. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @26:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1581s)
- Exit rule: once MACD crosses into negative territory during pullback, exit the trade. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @27:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=1649s)
- Monitor MACD crossover as exit signal when momentum shows signs of peaking. — [`aIqF9OvWYZ4` @10:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aIqF9OvWYZ4&t=638s)
- Do not trade MACD negative. Only trade when MACD is positive. [MACD crossover is sell signal]. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @61:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=3674s)
- Exit signals: gravestone doji rejection candles, high-volume selloff, or MACD crossover bearish. Track on intraday (1-minute and 5-minute). — [`dtAnMp8siUY` @7:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dtAnMp8siUY&t=435s)
- Monitor MACD for negative signals; if MACD goes negative while you're in a position, reduce or exit. — [`nM2MxYk6Eo0` @4:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nM2MxYk6Eo0&t=241s)
- Trade only on front side of move (above 20 EMA and MACD positive); exit immediately when moving average crosses negative or MACD flips. — [`p1W8Mjl4kWs` @30:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=p1W8Mjl4kWs&t=1813s)

### volume confirmation (2)

- During lighter volume (after 10:30 AM): reduce share size or exit strategy; difficult to scalp on light volume. — [`SpnidshD8Zk` @23:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SpnidshD8Zk&t=1387s)
- VWAP (volume-weighted average price) as third profit target level. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @10:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=612s)

### hotkeys (4)

- Keep hand on hotkeys (Ctrl+Z) positioned to exit immediately upon hesitation. — [`1axXKjgCe84` @6:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1axXKjgCe84&t=362s)
- Use a sell-half hotkey (not sell-full) when taking early profits on winners, holding remainder for bigger moves. [00:20:39] — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @20:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=1252s)
- Hot key sell order: Control + Z = sell full position at bid minus 15 cents. — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @13:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=808s)
- Hot key alternative sell: Control + K/L = sell at ask for strength selling. — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @13:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=822s)

### emotional control (3)

- Exit discipline: "breakout or bailout" philosophy—if trade doesn't work immediately, exit; don't hold losers. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @54:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=3249s)
- Exit discipline: Get green and shut down; don't overstay welcome [00:13:57] — [`LdLtyeZHbPU` @13:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LdLtyeZHbPU&t=831s)
- Exit discipline: walk away with profit rather than risk giving back large gains; one $1 flush on 4,000 shares = $4,000 loss. — [`gA8r-ZoN49Q` @7:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=gA8r-ZoN49Q&t=464s)

### accuracy / win rate (4)

- Target 70% accuracy (7 green days out of 10). — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @27:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=1677s)
- Accuracy target: 75% (exceeded on this challenge) [00:13:35] — [`ZRRCyIoZnHE` @13:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZRRCyIoZnHE&t=812s)
- Target accuracy of 75%. — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @4:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=287s)
- Focus on 15 to 20 cent base hit winners on momentum stocks (72% accuracy target). — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @5:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=345s)

### daily loss limit (2)

- Exit signal: if you have three consecutive losses, walk away; do not continue trading. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @26:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=1561s)
- Exit and walk away after giving back half of unrealized profit on the day. — [`vOZGdT9nYbc` @1:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vOZGdT9nYbc&t=111s)

### high of day break (4)

- First profit target: retest of high of day. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @1:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=91s)
- Target is a retest of high of day. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @22:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1354s)
- Profit target: retest of the high of day. — [`iIC62xnblLc` @33:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iIC62xnblLc&t=2019s)

### position sizing by risk (3)

- After a red day, focus on keeping position sizes small and walking away once you've made a target profit, rather than overtrading to recover losses. — [`9VAKgj1Px3s` @8:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VAKgj1Px3s&t=485s)
- Start each day with maximum 5,000-share position size until profit target ($1,000+) reached. — [`mBT72-bFlPM` @26:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mBT72-bFlPM&t=1615s)
- Target modest daily goal: 20 cents per share (scale position sizing around this). — [`nH-z-089CgM` @25:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nH-z-089CgM&t=1539s)

### halts (2)

- Exit before close call and volatility (halts, gaps); quit sooner before giving back half profits. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @21:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=1262s)
- On halt-down scenarios and big whips ($2-3 per share), exit position to avoid multi-million dollar loss. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @21:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=1317s)

### first candle to make a new low (2)

- Exit signal: first candle that makes a new low during profit-taking. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @42:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=2537s)
- Exit (short): close below 20 EMA or first candle to make new low below flag. — [`Xdw5azEqs6o`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xdw5azEqs6o)

### trailing stop (2)

- Do not hold entire position through first move in strong market; take half at 10% and trail the rest. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @24:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1474s)
- If sold half at 10%, sell another half at 15%, then trail the last piece. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @30:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1852s)

### first candle to make a new high (2)

- When stock breaks new high and starts squeezing, exit into the squeeze. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @13:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=787s)
- When price action fails to break new high after initial squeeze, exit the position (do not hold for another attempt). — [`q_ZliMvD8yc` @6:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=q_ZliMvD8yc&t=398s)

### uncategorised (51)

- Daily goal Phase 2: $25 risk / $25 profit target = 1:1 ratio. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @35:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2125s)
- Hold for final exit indicator; exit when trend exhausted. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @37:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2226s)
- Risk-to-reward target: if risking $1, should have potential to make $2 (1:2 ratio minimum; 1:2.5 or better preferred). — [`3rEakODkiEg` @14:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=897s)
- Never sit on the bid waiting for someone to sell—use ask when momentum is present. — [`4syXgXshgsc` @5:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=352s)
- Thinkorswim simulator execution: Took 11 seconds to fill a simple market sell order (speed test). — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @13:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=822s)
- For active trading beginners: target $200 profit per day from one trade (1,000 shares at 20 cents per share gains). — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @24:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1448s)
- For sell orders, offset 10 cents BELOW the bid to accommodate upward slippage on exit. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @5:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=341s)
- Exit when Heikin-Ashi shows first candle going red; do not wait for full candle close. — [`Ai6r6xjk8Uk` @14:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ai6r6xjk8Uk&t=849s)
- Second target: continuation higher if stock breaks through first target. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @1:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=97s)
- Target stocks already up minimum 10% on the day; this filters 8,000 equities down to ~50 candidates. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @15:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=902s)
- Target 15 cents per share as base hit profit goal rather than chasing home runs. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @14:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=872s)
- Risk $100 to potentially make $200 minimum (2-to-1 profit-loss ratio is target). — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @25:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=1552s)
- Breakout or bailout: if stock doesn't break out, exit immediately; no guessing. — [`GMRmMf-RsfE` @14:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=GMRmMf-RsfE&t=874s)
- "Breakout or bailout": if no immediate squeeze after breakout, exit. Do not hold and hope or swing for big winners on small account. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @34:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2082s)
- Small green days focus — Target $2,000-$3,000 daily goal rather than large swings. — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @2:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=158s)
- Profit target = 2x the risk amount. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @49:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=2941s)
- Sell/exit signal: green line crosses back down below red line; alligator stops eating and is ready to sleep. — [`NE0IxWksuqo` @4:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NE0IxWksuqo&t=242s)
- Require a 2-to-1 profit-loss ratio minimum on trades; if you risk $1.10 per share, you must target $2.20 per share profit potential. — [`OLvNqb3-xnY` @7:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLvNqb3-xnY&t=432s)
- If drops more than 5%, sell the rest of position. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @3:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=183s)
- Three strikes rule: exit strategy after three consecutive losses on the same stock. — [`RUuJzmz5jCg` @4:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RUuJzmz5jCg&t=255s)
- Exit strategy: when a stock has shown pop-and-drop behavior repeatedly, reduce risk by not adding aggressively on subsequent chances, even if levels look good. — [`SCy6RrASpJY` @44:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SCy6RrASpJY&t=2687s)
- Risk approximately 10 cents per share on each scalp trade; target 15-20 cent profit. — [`SpnidshD8Zk` @9:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SpnidshD8Zk&t=581s)
- Calculate annual target: If averaging $3,000 over 250 trading days = $800,000 year. — [`VSs11mUjdFg` @6:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VSs11mUjdFg&t=408s)
- [01:19:10-01:19:13] Target share prices between 2 and 10 dollars, but not more than 20 dollars. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)
- Exit: Sell when a red candle closes during an uptrend (first red candle is exit signal for existing position). — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @45:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=2700s)
- False breakout avoidance: If a trade doesn't work immediately, exit (breakout or bailout mentality). — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @52:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=3177s)
- Gravestone doji at top of squeeze = sell signal. Appears after series of green candles topping out. — [`Ymzyd9fbsmI` @1:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ymzyd9fbsmI&t=100s)
- When trading becomes more profitable than full-time job plus creates environmental stress, exit the job. — [`bnnEm_DKwXE` @18:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bnnEm_DKwXE&t=1084s)
- Cover partial position into dips after immediate profit, not requiring full position exit. — [`c5HU_4ukSw8` @4:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=c5HU_4ukSw8&t=285s)
- Minimum profit target: $200 per trade (20 cents per share × 1,000 shares). — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @5:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=300s)

## position-sizing (231 rules)

### position sizing by risk (113)

- Position sizing Phase 2: 50 share starter; increase to 100 shares after +$10 profit; scale up after achieving daily targets. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @34:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2082s)
- Phase 3 execution: repeat Phase 2 proof of concept plan with real money on same share size. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @39:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2398s)
- Step 7: Scale share size gradually; start 100 shares, increase to 200 if good result, then 400, 800. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @45:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2756s)
- Scale shares before scaling trade count; maximize share size on one trade per day first. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @46:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2808s)
- Position sizing: Scale share size up proportionally as account grows, but accept smaller percentage returns on larger accounts. — [`-N1qf9G3k5c` @3:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-N1qf9G3k5c&t=181s)
- Share size progression example: 100 shares making $20 = $0.20 per share profit. Scale to 1,000 shares = $200. Scale to 10,000 shares = $2,000 from same per-share profit. — [`-N1qf9G3k5c` @12:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-N1qf9G3k5c&t=735s)
- Starting account checkpoint: Once profitable with small consistent strategy, slowly increase position size. — [`-N1qf9G3k5c` @10:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-N1qf9G3k5c&t=601s)
- Sweet spot for position sizing: stocks between 5 and 10 dollars. Will trade 2–20 but avoid trading under 2 or over 20 dollars in normal conditions. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @4:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=264s)
- Position sizing: use hotkey set to 15% of buying power to automatically calculate share count for each trade. — [`0Zh105lq9Rk` @2:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0Zh105lq9Rk&t=121s)
- Trade with smaller share size (100-3,000 shares) to reduce slippage on bail exits. — [`1axXKjgCe84` @13:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1axXKjgCe84&t=802s)
- Maximum position size should not exceed account size that creates fear-based emotional trading. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @4:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=272s)
- Sweet spot price range for maximum position size and profit potential: $5 to $10 per share. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @5:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=330s)
- Minimum share size goal: 1,000 shares to justify commission costs. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @6:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=379s)
- Throttle up share size in hot markets; throttle down in cold markets (like driving a racetrack). — [`2higxJXgl6o` @11:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=682s)
- Do not increase share size due to emotional response; only increase based on actual market conditions. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @12:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=770s)
- Do not apply hot-market share size to stocks showing weakness (red doji at high of day). — [`2higxJXgl6o` @13:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=803s)
- Never use leverage on stocks under $5/share if broker restricts it; plan for smaller share size. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @10:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=627s)
- With leverage: press hotkey (Shift 1) to calculate auto share size (90% of buying power). — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @11:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=688s)
- Max position size limiter: set in broker settings to force discipline and prevent oversizing during emotional trades. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @22:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=1360s)
- Trade below your skill level (smaller position sizes than intuition suggests) until proven profitable over 18+ months. — [`5AV2OLWD1gc` @3:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5AV2OLWD1gc&t=210s)
- Minimum share size goal of 1,000 shares for viable profit targets. — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @23:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1418s)
- First trade: use 5,000 share maximum until up $1,000 on the day; then reassess position sizing. — [`6w-7geIsq1A` @14:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6w-7geIsq1A&t=858s)
- Position sizing during aggressive trading: scale down share size when chasing (signal of deviation from plan). — [`8eLtork_M50` @38:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8eLtork_M50&t=2322s)
- In May reversal strategy: limit share size to 2,500 shares maximum per trade instead of trading 20,000-25,000 shares. — [`DoO4YnbPOPA` @15:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoO4YnbPOPA&t=919s)
- Position sizing: scale up only after hitting profit targets; do not maintain full size on every trade. — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @42:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=2522s)
- June turnaround rule: trade only "A-quality" setups but take double normal share size on these high-conviction trades. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @46:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2761s)
- Reduce position size (from 4000 to 3000 shares) as price gets extended to manage risk. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @16:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=962s)
- 5,000 shares max — Position size cap until cushion rebuilt. — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @1:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=89s)
- Starting share size: 10,000 shares for cheaper stocks (<$1 per share). — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @16:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=1003s)
- STEP 2: Apply account restrictions—reduce max share size to 1/4 normal (5,000 shares instead of 20,000). — [`Hq3sZh5B_l8` @1:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Hq3sZh5B_l8&t=115s)

### profit-loss ratio 2:1 (16)

- Trade 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio minimum. If accuracy around 65%, this ratio ensures profitability. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @40:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=2407s)
- Use a 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio minimum: if you risk $1, aim to make $2 back to set the bar lower for accuracy. — [`5AV2OLWD1gc` @6:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5AV2OLWD1gc&t=404s)
- Risk $1 to make $2 (2:1 profit-to-loss ratio) to only need 33% accuracy for break-even. — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @13:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=818s)
- Risk-reward framework: risk $700 to make $1,500-1,500 profit = 2:1 ratio; need only 33% win rate to breakeven. — [`JMcaRfFThmg` @1:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JMcaRfFThmg&t=60s)
- Risk 10 cents per share to make 20 cents per share (2:1 profit to loss ratio). — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @16:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=961s)
- [02:44:36-02:45:16] Aim for a minimum 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio; a 1:1 ratio requires 50% accuracy to break even. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)
- Risk-to-reward ratio: Risk $100 to make $200 minimum (2:1); only need 33% accuracy to break even. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @17:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1043s)
- Realistic win rate: Aim for 50%+ accuracy; 33% with 2:1 ratio just breaks even psychologically. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @17:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1048s)
- Aim for 2:1 risk-to-reward ratio: risk $1 to make $2. — [`_fsRWw0VbS4` @17:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_fsRWw0VbS4&t=1072s)
- Profit-to-loss ratio minimum: Risk 1 unit to make 2 units; break-even accuracy is 33% with 2:1 ratio. [00:38:02] — [`d0wt45LbvWo` @50:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d0wt45LbvWo&t=3051s)
- Profit-to-loss ratio: must have 2:1 potential before entering trade. — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @26:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=1565s)
- Take only trades where you stand to gain at least twice what you are risking (2:1 profit-to-loss ratio minimum). — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @7:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=436s)
- Month 1-3: aim for 10 trades per day, 50% success rate, 2:1 profit-loss ratio; gradually increase risk from $50 to $200 per trade. — [`mbIwWiLY4DQ` @21:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mbIwWiLY4DQ&t=1281s)
- For a 2:1 profit-to-loss ratio, you only need to be right 33% of the time to break even. — [`oKlhUSSHe2Q` @14:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=oKlhUSSHe2Q&t=841s)
- Use a 2:1 profit/loss ratio as minimum for trade justification. — [`sv6U1nAO8BI` @25:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sv6U1nAO8BI&t=1559s)
- Profit-to-loss ratio: minimum 2:1 (if risking $10, profit potential must be $20+). — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @27:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=1652s)

### emotional control (6)

- Avoid trading outside routine: Do not trade while on vacation, during off-hours, or when emotionally triggered by external stress. [00:15:32] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @34:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=2077s)
- Mindfulness-based framework (RAIN): Recognition (what's happening), Acceptance (it is what it is), Investigation (inquiry), Non-attachment (step back emotionally). [00:28:38] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @38:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=2283s)
- Journaling rule: Track emotional composure before each trade (scale 1–7) in an Excel document to correlate emotional state with performance. [00:30:36] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @30:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=1836s)
- Acupuncture (experimental): Breathing through physical discomfort as emotional regulation tool post-loss. [00:39:39] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @39:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=2379s)
- Consecutive days discipline: Focus on maintaining discipline for 30-40-50 days in a row rather than achieving green days consecutively [00:21:27] — [`mfGQr2tHoX0` @18:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mfGQr2tHoX0&t=1087s)
- [00:01:56-00:02:05] Item 1: Did you eat well and sleep well? Impacts discipline when needed — [`nrrH3eISme0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nrrH3eISme0)

### accuracy / win rate (6)

- Break-even accuracy: 33% win rate with 1:2 risk-reward breaks even; 50% win rate goal; 65-70% accuracy is strong. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @15:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=904s)
- Risk $1 to make $1 (1:1 ratio) requires 50% accuracy for break-even. — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @13:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=826s)
- Time-of-day strategy: Trade between 7:00 a.m. - 11:00 a.m. Eastern (based on data analysis of win rates). — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @31:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1888s)
- On three-to-one profit-loss ratio, accuracy needs to be only 25-30% to break even; on 2-to-1 ratio, 33% accuracy suffices. — [`YiMItz4fgFk` @29:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YiMItz4fgFk&t=1742s)
- 1.5-to-1 profit-loss ratio requires approximately 41% accuracy to be profitable. — [`YiMItz4fgFk` @31:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YiMItz4fgFk&t=1875s)
- Require 50% accuracy minimum at 1:1 ratio to break even. — [`yI_a4pJj_go` @12:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yI_a4pJj_go&t=720s)

### first hour / market open (10)

- Trade primarily from 7:00 a.m. to 10:00 a.m., approximately 2-3 hours daily during market open window. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @16:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=987s)
- Candlestick charts represent different time periods: each candle on a 1-minute chart represents 1 minute of price action; each candle on a daily chart represents one full day (9:30 a.m. to 4:00 p.m. ET). — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @8:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=480s)
- Trade the first two hours of market open (8:30-10:00 a.m., extended to 11:00 on hot days). — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @2:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=141s)
- Trade for approximately one hour per day (9:30-10:30am ET). — [`_NAM_oTYUQM` @16:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_NAM_oTYUQM&t=1012s)
- Market opens at 9:30 AM ET; usually done trading by 10:30 AM (about 1 hour). — [`_NAM_oTYUQM` @9:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_NAM_oTYUQM&t=571s)
- Avoid trading first five minutes after market open (9:30-9:35 ET): volatility too extreme (2-3 point swings common) to manage tight risk. — [`dtAnMp8siUY` @2:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dtAnMp8siUY&t=170s)
- Market orders work from 9:30 a.m. until 4 p.m.; limit orders work from 4:00 a.m. until 8:00 p.m. depending on broker (thinkorswim: 7:00 a.m. to 8:00 p.m.). — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @5:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=322s)
- Trade only during highest-success periods based on historical data (for the instructor: 9:30-11:30 AM). — [`mbIwWiLY4DQ` @8:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mbIwWiLY4DQ&t=490s)
- Trade during first hour of day (9:30-10:30am) for highest volume and volatility. — [`txWaMpSzHhM` @1:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=txWaMpSzHhM&t=76s)
- Most brokers require extended-hours order designation (separate from day order 9:30-4pm). — [`y-sFO1ouTX4` @52:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y-sFO1ouTX4&t=3152s)

### midday avoidance (3)

- Trade in morning window between 7:00 a.m. and 11:00 a.m. for best results. — [`G4uTdr_zx1I` @11:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=G4uTdr_zx1I&t=701s)
- Trade between 8:00 a.m. and 10:00 a.m., potentially extending to 11:00 a.m. in hot markets. — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @7:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=421s)

### reversal (4)

- When candle fully outside Bollinger Bands, expect reversal coming soon (not guaranteed, but high likelihood). [00:03:14] — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @3:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=192s)

### uncategorised (62)

- Not trading third pullback; early pullbacks (first, second) have best risk-reward; third pullback often signals end of move. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @38:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=2334s)
- [00:12:32-00:12:48] Cup and handle variant: after initial pop and pullback, cup forms, then handle pullback before final breakout — [`4jes2_N3m2I`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4jes2_N3m2I)
- Initial scan review: Look for gaps of at least 20% or higher (prefer over 5% minimum) [00:09:00] — [`7RbjGk9eNJU` @7:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7RbjGk9eNJU&t=425s)
- Price filter: Typically trade stocks between $2 and $20 per share (sweet spot) [00:17:14] — [`7RbjGk9eNJU` @7:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7RbjGk9eNJU&t=469s)
- First setup: First pullback after initial spike breakout [00:14:05] — [`7RbjGk9eNJU` @14:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7RbjGk9eNJU&t=873s)
- Do not overstay welcome; aim for 1-2 high-quality trades daily [00:18:44] — [`7RbjGk9eNJU` @14:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7RbjGk9eNJU&t=875s)
- Risk management: Give back ~15% of daily profit off top, then consider walking away [00:19:28] — [`7RbjGk9eNJU` @19:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7RbjGk9eNJU&t=1186s)
- Time window: 7:00 a.m. to 10:00 a.m. EST only; no trades after 10 a.m. for small account. — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @18:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=1136s)
- Use moving average 200 (exponential) on daily chart at 2:00 line width, magenta color for visibility. — [`C4XZ_67c0Fo` @4:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=C4XZ_67c0Fo&t=289s)
- [00:01:14-00:01:19] Blue sky setup: stock breaks through all-time highs, the highest price it has ever traded — [`D8Guwf84eAA`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=D8Guwf84eAA)
- Keep Bollinger Band settings standard (do not customize); matches what other traders see. [00:02:08] — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @2:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=126s)
- Nearly 95% of price action daily occurs inside Bollinger Bands. [00:05:08] — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @5:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=307s)
- Trade between 7:00 a.m. and 10:00 a.m. ET window for best profit probability. — [`E2SYZEF5AEs` @32:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=E2SYZEF5AEs&t=1963s)
- Size up to 20,000 shares after building cushion on single stock. — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @17:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=1024s)
- [00:01:14-00:01:18] Point B: first candle to make new high but does NOT break through high of day — [`IHeJROuEzRU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IHeJROuEzRU)
- [00:02:35-00:02:39] Point C: pullback cannot go lower than point A; can double bottom — [`IHeJROuEzRU`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IHeJROuEzRU)
- Meditation practice: Daily meditation to build comfort with discomfort (prerequisite for handling market stress). [00:36:23] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @38:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=2280s)
- Exercise routine: Regular physical exercise increases mental composure capacity. [00:38:56] — [`JSqI99gL_KA` @39:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JSqI99gL_KA&t=2340s)
- Verify internet speed before booking travel accommodation: Use speedtest.net to confirm speed [00:01:47] — [`LdLtyeZHbPU` @1:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LdLtyeZHbPU&t=110s)
- Pack traveling trading station in carry-on only (never checked luggage) [00:05:12] — [`LdLtyeZHbPU` @4:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LdLtyeZHbPU&t=244s)
- Minimum equipment: 1 laptop + 2 USB monitors [00:07:15] — [`LdLtyeZHbPU` @4:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LdLtyeZHbPU&t=254s)
- Trading window: Limit to 2-hour trading window maximum when traveling [00:13:18] — [`LdLtyeZHbPU` @10:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LdLtyeZHbPU&t=659s)
- Minimum profit/loss ratio in phase one: 1:1 (average winners equal average losers in value). — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @28:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=1731s)
- First trade goal: Make $1,000 on the first trade to build cushion before sizing up. — [`VSs11mUjdFg` @15:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VSs11mUjdFg&t=906s)
- Base hit profit taking: Take small profits (10-15 cents) quickly rather than waiting for 50-cent moves. [00:22:45] — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @14:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=871s)
- Breakout or bailout philosophy: Immediate resolution expected; if stalls, likely wrong timing. [00:19:59] — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @22:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=1377s)
- [02:47:45-02:47:59] Stick to predetermined risk boundaries during live trading; do not change risk parameters mid-trade to accommodate losers. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)
- [03:16:36-03:16:42] Trade the market you're in: throttle up in hot markets with larger positions, throttle down in cold markets. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)
- [03:55:05-03:55:08] Daily goal is to pull 10 to 15 cents per share out of the market as a minimum. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)
- [03:55:05-03:56:03] This 10-15 cents-per-share goal is achievable, scalable (e.g., 100 shares = $10/day, 1,000 shares = $100/day, 5,000 shares = $500/day), and separates professional consistent traders from all others. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0`](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0)

## risk-limits (28 rules)

### daily loss limit (16)

- Walk away if you give back more than half of your day's gains. Lock in at least half your profits. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @9:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=570s)
- Set internal circuit breaker when FOMO triggers are hit; walk away from trading. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @15:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=910s)
- RULE 2: If you go red on the day (from positive to negative), walk away immediately. — [`H9BRF4xynQA` @9:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=H9BRF4xynQA&t=563s)
- Walk away before close call; quit before giving back half of profits when halts/gaps present. — [`HY7i78bOJFA` @14:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HY7i78bOJFA&t=893s)
- Recognize when a stock profile (choppy, grinding micro-moves) doesn't match your edge; walk away to avoid emotional hijack. — [`RUuJzmz5jCg` @15:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RUuJzmz5jCg&t=910s)
- Walk away with cushion rather than chase; emotional discipline is more valuable than margin. — [`RUuJzmz5jCg` @21:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RUuJzmz5jCg&t=1283s)
- Daily maximum loss is 6% of account ($60 on day one with $1,000). — [`RoAVk--vljU` @6:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RoAVk--vljU&t=400s)
- When you've squeezed as much as you can from market, don't keep going back; walk away to lock in profits. — [`Uft2BuJpap8` @4:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Uft2BuJpap8&t=270s)
- Profit protection: Walk away if you give back half your daily profit at any point. — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @39:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=2340s)
- Walk away and lock up profit after a successful run rather than risk giving back gains. — [`aIqF9OvWYZ4` @20:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aIqF9OvWYZ4&t=1229s)
- If you give back 50% of profit above daily goal, walk away. — [`anuxv488waA` @6:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=anuxv488waA&t=373s)
- For Warrior founder: trade morning hours (early-to-11am) only, then shut down charts completely; do not trade afternoon. — [`bnnEm_DKwXE` @16:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bnnEm_DKwXE&t=992s)
- Max daily loss equals your daily profit goal (same magnitude, opposite direction). — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @66:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=3975s)
- Set maximum daily loss equal to daily profit goal (e.g., $5,000 loss cap with $5,000 profit goal). — [`mBT72-bFlPM` @27:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mBT72-bFlPM&t=1678s)
- Recognize emotional tipping points (frustrated, desperate, seeing red); walk away immediately. — [`qPb8LF1k8vU` @13:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qPb8LF1k8vU&t=813s)
- Trade until your first loss, then walk away (beginner strategy). — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @35:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=2128s)

### max loss per trade (3)

- In May reversal strategy: keep stops tight with maximum loss of $500 per trade. — [`DoO4YnbPOPA` @15:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoO4YnbPOPA&t=946s)
- Risk management: never risk more than 3–5% of account per trade. — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @26:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=1583s)
- Limit maximum loss per trade to $1,000; if a single trade is down more than $1,000, market order out immediately. — [`yKV3C2DoaFg` @33:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yKV3C2DoaFg&t=1997s)

### uncategorised (9)

- Weekly goal = daily goal times three. — [`Bqo2XGDZtVU` @43:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Bqo2XGDZtVU&t=2587s)
- Initial daily goal: 15-20 cents per share profit per day. — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @7:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=447s)
- Rule of thumb for trading halts: trade after first halt, trade after second halt, but do not trade after third halt or beyond. — [`StpXbe3Ga3Y` @19:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=StpXbe3Ga3Y&t=1181s)
- After hitting daily goal, only risk up to the day's cushion amount on subsequent trades. — [`V9za4wt9ONo` @17:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=V9za4wt9ONo&t=1063s)
- Daily goal: Aim for $2,500-$3,000 per day consistently rather than chasing volatile large wins. — [`VSs11mUjdFg` @6:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VSs11mUjdFg&t=360s)
- After reaching daily goal: size back down to preserve profit (not continue aggressive sizing). — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @26:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=1606s)
- Daily goal in cold market is $5,000. — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @30:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=1812s)
- Daily goal in hot market is $20,000. — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @30:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=1815s)
- Hit daily goal in first 30 minutes with 1-2 trades, then ease off. — [`txWaMpSzHhM` @2:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=txWaMpSzHhM&t=122s)

## timing (6 rules)

### uncategorised (6)

- Do not trade afternoon hours unless specific conditions are met. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @1:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=90s)
- Question decision-making: am I filtering data emotionally or accepting it objectively. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @13:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=803s)
- Lock up profit within the first 30-minute window; this is the highest-confidence period before MACD crossover. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @19:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1142s)
- Trick 6: Pay attention to time of day; candlestick patterns most reliable during high volume periods. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @33:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1995s)
- Power hour: 3:00pm-4:00pm; traders adjusting overnight positions. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @34:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2054s)
- Only trade during optimal window; previously focused on morning hours, eventually narrowed to 7 a.m. to 11 a.m. — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @43:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=2625s)

## patterns (141 rules)

### reversal (43)

- A doji is a reversal warning when it comes after strong uptrend; confirmation is if the next candle goes lower. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @14:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=848s)
- Ascending support line draw: connect lows to identify technical support; break of line signals potential reversal/selloff. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @5:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=358s)
- Use one-minute MACD as filter: do not take trades when one-minute MACD goes negative; wait for reversal. — [`DAnpW98ZCSg` @9:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DAnpW98ZCSg&t=590s)
- Multiple reversal indicators required: Price extended outside BB + extended off 9 EMA + MACD approaching crossover. — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @8:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=507s)
- Candle fully outside upper band = doji or spinning top pattern = very high reversal likelihood. — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @11:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=664s)
- In May reversal strategy: focus on buying quality pullback setups rather than chasing stocks squeezing up. — [`DoO4YnbPOPA` @15:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DoO4YnbPOPA&t=959s)
- Bull flag is often same as failed ABCD pattern (reversal trade on break). — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @32:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1925s)
- RSI overbought threshold: above 80 (or extended to 90s) signals potential reversal. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @45:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=2728s)
- Candle outside Bollinger bands that closes back inside = reversal signal. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @52:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=3165s)
- Watch for signs of weakness: big seller appearing on level two, hidden seller, large burst of red on tape, dramatic reversal with red candle close. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @32:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1926s)
- Trick 4: Remember reversal candles; they signal potential trend changes. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @18:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1085s)
- Gravestone doji: longer upper wick; more bearish than standard doji; signals reversal. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @20:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1225s)
- These reversal candles gain significance only in context of big move up or big move down. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @21:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1298s)
- When reversal candle appears at top of move, expects short-term correction. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @21:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1305s)
- Hammer candle: bottoming tail at base; signals bullish reversal; place order on ask for confirmation candle. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @24:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1473s)
- Candle over candle (two-candle pattern): first candle warning, next candle confirms reversal. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @26:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1575s)
- Support/resistance reversal: Prior resistance becomes support when price moves above it and pulls back. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @14:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=874s)
- Confirmation on dragonfly doji: next candle should move higher to confirm reversal. — [`Ymzyd9fbsmI` @6:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ymzyd9fbsmI&t=408s)
- On ABCD pattern, do not break the low of the second pullback—if it does, the pattern fails and trend reverses. — [`_4Q0hlNFn-A` @40:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4Q0hlNFn-A&t=2415s)
- For reversal trades, short when price extends far from the moving average, targeting the 9 EMA on 5-minute or 20 EMA on 1-minute for the cover level. — [`aC7jCwYYHxY` @13:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aC7jCwYYHxY&t=785s)
- Use Bollinger Bands as a reversal filter: short when price trades outside the Bollinger Bands, covering on the move back inside. — [`aC7jCwYYHxY` @20:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aC7jCwYYHxY&t=1218s)
- Wait for reversal confirmation (closing candle below resistance) before covering short; do not cover into the strength itself. — [`c5HU_4ukSw8` @15:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=c5HU_4ukSw8&t=927s)
- High-volume flush candle pattern: if resolves down with high-volume red candle, pattern is broken/reversal confirmed. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @11:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=703s)
- Tap-tap-tap-break pattern warning: if stock taps resistance multiple times (3rd, 4th attempt) before breaking, each failed tap = more resistance; breaks on 4th tap by only 2 cents = immediate reversal likely. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @15:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=920s)
- Confirmation required: red candle breaking below previous candle low (candle-under-candle) confirms reversal. — [`dvetF0H3pNo` @35:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dvetF0H3pNo&t=2157s)
- Only trade when MACD is above its signal line (green histogram); avoid trades when MACD crosses below signal line (consolidation/reversal). — [`eTUYXkAr6Pc` @21:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eTUYXkAr6Pc&t=1273s)
- Divergence pattern: when price makes new high but RSI fails to make new high, expect reversal soon. — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @13:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=807s)
- Short reversal setup: price outside Bollinger Bands + RSI >70 + candle showing weakness (e.g., red candle). — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @15:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=901s)
- Look for doji at bottom of selloff as reversal signal; two consecutive red candles indicate potential reversal setup. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @7:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=452s)
- Five to ten consecutive red candles ending with pin bar/doji at bottom = strong reversal signal. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @25:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=1538s)
- _...13 more._

### volume confirmation (14)

- Pattern recognition: high volume on move up, light volume on pullback, higher volume on next move up. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @30:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=1840s)
- Volume bars must be colored by candle close/open; never use monochrome volume. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @8:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=503s)
- Light volume on pullback indicates buyers are resting, not panic selling. — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @31:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=1870s)
- High volume on green candles during initial move; light volume on red candles during pullback confirms pattern validity. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @6:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=406s)
- Volume profile: high volume move up + light volume pullback = likely continuation up; high volume selling + light volume up = likely decline. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @15:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=902s)
- Volume bars: color-code to match candle (green candle = green volume bar, red candle = red volume bar). — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @37:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2267s)
- Prefer high volume on green candles and light volume on red candles during the pullback to confirm buyer strength. — [`HYoQYCBW4sw` @33:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HYoQYCBW4sw&t=1982s)
- Bull flag structure: must have high volume on green candles, light volume on red candles, then high volume on breakout green. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @16:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1001s)
- Bull flag rule: must hold above VWAP (volume weighted average price); break below VWAP signals weakness. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @16:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1015s)
- Wait for a new high of day on heavy buying volume before entering a flag breakout at resistance or trendline break. — [`VNsh-8cS2Tg` @9:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VNsh-8cS2Tg&t=566s)
- Trade the break of volume-weighted average price after consolidation pullback. — [`aIqF9OvWYZ4` @9:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aIqF9OvWYZ4&t=597s)
- First candle break volume: if breakout candle has medium/green volume bigger than prior red candles, likely resolves higher; if lighter volume, false breakout more probable. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @11:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=690s)
- Volume profile during a strong move: high volume on the move up, light volume on pullback, then higher volume on next move up. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @1:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=81s)
- High volume required on green candles, light volume on red candles in a proper bull flag. — [`xgnqOu_fchA` @6:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xgnqOu_fchA&t=360s)

### bull flag (10)

- Look for clean candlestick patterns (bull flag, clean pullbacks); avoid trading when topping tails appear. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @8:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=491s)
- Bull flag pattern: sharp move up, 2-3 candle pullback close to 9 EMA, then break of prior high. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @28:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1726s)
- The pullback on a bull flag should not retrace more than 50% of the initial move; favor pullbacks staying within the top 25% of the range. — [`HYoQYCBW4sw` @34:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HYoQYCBW4sw&t=2044s)
- Trade the first pullback (bull flag) when MACD is positive. — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @34:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=2040s)
- Bull flag retracement rule: should not retrace more than 50% of initial move. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @16:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1007s)
- Bull flag pullback should not retrace more than 50% of the upswing. — [`UNoPqBWuLC0` @10:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UNoPqBWuLC0&t=621s)
- Good price action shows clean breakouts with traditional patterns (bull flag); poor shows rejections and false breakouts. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @0:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=34s)
- Bull flag pattern: pullback should consist of one to three candles maximum; more than three indicates loss of strength. — [`ul34Jfh-LOk` @12:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ul34Jfh-LOk&t=733s)
- Bull flag pullback may not retrace more than 50% of the initial move; retracing beyond 50% breaks the pattern. — [`ul34Jfh-LOk` @12:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ul34Jfh-LOk&t=748s)
- Bull flag pullback should retrace no more than 50% of the preceding move. — [`xgnqOu_fchA` @6:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xgnqOu_fchA&t=414s)

### support and resistance (6)

- ABCD pattern: leg up, pullback, second leg up but failing to make new high; watch for break back up through resistance. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @31:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1862s)
- Ascending support trend line: drawn by connecting lows; used to identify support in ABCD patterns. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @33:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1986s)
- 9 EMA usage: acts as support level on intraday charts; entering on pullback to 9 EMA is bullish. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @13:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=781s)
- Starter position: Take small initial position at support; don't wait for top of pullback. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @21:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1268s)
- Shadow Theory: Everything behind a candle has been broken; previous resistance/support at that price is invalidated. — [`YZ5nvP7DnQQ` @1:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YZ5nvP7DnQQ&t=110s)
- When price breaks through a previous support/resistance level on a daily candle, all historical price action behind that candle is invalidated for future resistance/support purposes. — [`YZ5nvP7DnQQ` @2:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YZ5nvP7DnQQ&t=133s)

### abcd pattern (6)

- ABCD pattern: pop up, pull back, pop back up, pull back; base risk off low of second pullback and VWAP. — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @4:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=259s)
- ABCD pattern: acceptable second pullback variation (move up, pullback, move up again = two attempts). — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @43:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=2595s)
- ABCD pattern critical rule: pullback cannot break below the low of first pullback or it becomes bearish stairstepping. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @66:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=3964s)
- Point C (second pullback in ABCD) must not break below point A (first low); if it does, pattern is invalidated as stock is stair-stepping down. — [`xgnqOu_fchA` @8:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xgnqOu_fchA&t=501s)
- Do not trade ABCD patterns that extend beyond second attempt; repeated failures = weak stock. — [`xgnqOu_fchA` @37:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xgnqOu_fchA&t=2278s)
- For ABCD/W pattern: wait for break of the pullback low; if it breaks lower, no longer bullish. — [`y-sFO1ouTX4` @38:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y-sFO1ouTX4&t=2331s)

### moving average (9 EMA) (3)

- Use 9 EMA on all timeframes (1 minute, 5 minute, daily); perfect pullback touches 9 EMA then bounces. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @17:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1034s)
- Multi-timeframe alignment: 1-minute chart pullback to 20 EMA often aligns with 5-minute pullback to 9 EMA. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @60:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=3601s)
- After a big move and pullback, wait for price to come down closer to the 9 EMA or for the 9 EMA to come up closer to price before taking the next trade. — [`eTUYXkAr6Pc` @31:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eTUYXkAr6Pc&t=1875s)

### first candle to make a new high (3)

- Focus on micro pullback setups where first candle makes a new high on the one-minute chart after pullback. — [`OQLfHuO8KlI` @25:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OQLfHuO8KlI&t=1501s)
- Scaling strategy in hot market: starter position low, add on first candle to new high, add again on next high. — [`QzBogJmcl-c` @22:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QzBogJmcl-c&t=1347s)
- Close down the first candle to make new high following pullback if trade breaks down. — [`uoig-Z6qtV0` @15:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=uoig-Z6qtV0&t=952s)

### MACD (2)

- Trade setup: micro pullback on front side of breakout when MACD is above signal line. — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @14:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=844s)
- When MACD crosses below signal line, first leg of move may be over; pullback expected. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @23:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=1416s)

### micro pullback (4)

- Micro pullback: quick move up, brief pullback, continuation; occurs on 10-second chart appearing as straight up on 1-minute chart. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @26:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=1612s)
- Lock up profit on the green candle after strong micro pullback move. — [`MTbYDUswdbM` @3:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MTbYDUswdbM&t=228s)
- For micro pullbacks: expect very fast moves (often 40–50% up) before pullback forms; don't wait for clean 5-minute pullback. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @26:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=1561s)
- Take profits early on dips when using the micro-pullback strategy. — [`yFoBnM0iSlc` @34:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yFoBnM0iSlc&t=2048s)

### high of day break (2)

- Avoid buying at or near the high of day; wait for pullback and rip setup instead. — [`H9BRF4xynQA` @17:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=H9BRF4xynQA&t=1075s)
- High-day breakout: break of previous day's high or double-top break if previous pullback didn't hold. — [`P5Sn_mWJdy0` @8:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P5Sn_mWJdy0&t=519s)

### uncategorised (48)

- Upper candle wicks are bearish; lower candle wicks are bullish. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @16:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=998s)
- A hammer (candle with bottoming tail) is bullish because it shows price declined but buyers rallied it back up. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @16:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=1002s)
- A shooting star (candle with large upper wick) is bearish because it shows price squeezed up then got pushed down. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @18:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=1080s)
- Doji signals matter only during trend periods, not during sideways/indecisive price action. — [`3hmDWJfZ0_0` @14:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3hmDWJfZ0_0&t=854s)
- Pullback should not exceed 50% of initial move; ideal pullback is top 20-25% of the range. — [`4syXgXshgsc` @17:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=1057s)
- Pattern quality: pullback should not retrace more than 50% of initial move; if retraces >50%, price action is broken. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @5:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=334s)
- Secondary offering cycle: once bounce occurs, company may announce secondary offering, diluting stock and causing pullback. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @15:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=920s)
- Trade first and second pullback only; avoid third pullback (oversaying position increases risk). — [`8eLtork_M50` @28:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8eLtork_M50&t=1739s)
- Trade the first and second pullback only (highest win rate setups). — [`A3sbJBOLGuI` @26:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=A3sbJBOLGuI&t=1591s)
- Hold position until first Heikin-Ashi candle closes red or goes candle under candle (breaks low of prior candle). — [`Ai6r6xjk8Uk` @5:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ai6r6xjk8Uk&t=316s)
- Pullback maximum 25% of initial move preferred; 50% is hard limit. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @30:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=1807s)
- Do not trade if pullback is more than 50% of the initial move. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @37:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=2277s)
- Dip and rip strategy: anticipate panic sellers on halt resumption, then squeeze higher. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @23:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=1404s)
- When VWAP is reclaimed during pullback and bears lose control, expect strong squeeze higher. — [`G4uTdr_zx1I` @31:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=G4uTdr_zx1I&t=1874s)
