- Doji, spinning top, shooting star, hanging man: only problematic when they occur at end of strong move up (not during sideways/consolidation). — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @13:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=785s)
- When trading highly volatile stocks, focus on dips and curls (pullback-rips) rather than chasing the main leg up. — [`H9BRF4xynQA` @17:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=H9BRF4xynQA&t=1063s)
- Wait for green orders on time-and-sales window before entering; green trades indicate buying resumption after the pullback. — [`HYoQYCBW4sw` @35:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HYoQYCBW4sw&t=2151s)
- In pullback pattern: look for bottoming tail (shows buyers came in) before entering. — [`L16yPlr8jyM` @5:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L16yPlr8jyM&t=338s)
- Final hold: keep last 1/4 until first candle steps down (continues momentum). — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @41:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=2492s)
- Do not hold 3rd pullback (overexposure on exhausted setup). — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @54:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=3272s)
- Pullback should not exceed 50% of the initial move. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @18:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1139s)
- Doji candle: opens and closes at same price with upper and lower wicks; signals indecision. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @19:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1142s)
- Trick 5: Watch the clock on intraday candles; as candle closes, shape becomes solid; use remaining time to anticipate confirmation. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @28:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1685s)
- With 10 seconds left on candle, if next candle breaks price, you have confirmation. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @29:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1778s)
- Focus on last minute of five-minute candle or last few seconds of one-minute candle. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @30:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=1835s)
- Stocks must hold at least 50% of the initial move during pullback to be tradeable on the first pullback setup. — [`StpXbe3Ga3Y` @13:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=StpXbe3Ga3Y&t=809s)
- Pullback length: maximum 4-5 red candles before pattern deteriorates; traders lose interest in 5-6+ candle pullbacks. — [`UNoPqBWuLC0` @12:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UNoPqBWuLC0&t=740s)
- No doji candles allowed at top of pattern; shooting star (red close after spike) raises red flag. — [`UNoPqBWuLC0` @12:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UNoPqBWuLC0&t=766s)
- Use the one-minute chart for first-hour trading to identify flag breaks and tight stops. — [`VNsh-8cS2Tg` @7:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VNsh-8cS2Tg&t=454s)
- 50% retracement rule: If stock retraces more than 50% of initial move, pullback is too deep; prefer 25-75% retracement depth. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @10:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=604s)
- _...18 more._

## indicators (100 rules)

### MACD (30)

- Do not trade breakouts when MACD is against the trade (negative). — [`1FKu4LH0Xss` @4:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1FKu4LH0Xss&t=254s)
- After MACD crossover, expect choppy price action and avoid breakout trades. — [`1FKu4LH0Xss` @6:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1FKu4LH0Xss&t=375s)
- Focus on highest quality trades only; trade front-side of moves when MACD is pulling away, moving averages trending up. — [`1axXKjgCe84` @12:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1axXKjgCe84&t=754s)
- Trade ONLY when MACD is positive; do not trade when MACD is negative. Positive = easier, cleaner price action. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @19:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1143s)
- Use only essential indicators: 9 EMA, 20 EMA, 200 EMA, VWAP, volume bars, MACD. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @5:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=340s)
- Only trade when MACD is positive or crossing from negative to positive. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @8:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=481s) _(2 videos)_
- MACD is an oscillator that shows when moving averages are diverging (bullish) or converging (bearish). — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @1:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=98s)
- Do not trade MACD crossovers on stocks that have not proven they can move big (not top 5% gainers). — [`7PVpVDHYF2E` @41:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=7PVpVDHYF2E&t=2460s)
- MACD used only on 1-minute timeframe (signals on 5-minute too slow, on 10-second too fast); crossover indicates front side of move may be over. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @67:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=4024s)
- Trade MACD only when open and moving upward; avoid trading after MACD crossover into negative territory. — [`CSzDAEAoYqk` @7:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=CSzDAEAoYqk&t=467s)
- Do not hold past MACD crossover. — [`DP4ayEWhmvM` @9:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DP4ayEWhmvM&t=542s)
- MACD: trade when above signal line; when crosses below, expect choppier price action and lower profit probability. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @45:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2732s)
- MACD crosses back up: can take another trade if setup is strong; avoid most trades until it crosses back positive. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @48:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2915s)
- If MACD crosses negative, do not take the dip trade; higher likelihood of dump. — [`IwDORxvXAAs` @30:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IwDORxvXAAs&t=1815s)
- Remove all technical indicators and focus on candlesticks themselves; add only: VWAP, 9/20/200 EMA, volume bars, and MACD bonus. — [`L16yPlr8jyM` @0:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=L16yPlr8jyM&t=45s)
- MACD settings: 12, 26, 9 (standard calculation; do not change). — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @32:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=1920s)
- Only trade when MACD histogram is green (positive); avoid trading when MACD is negative/red. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @35:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=2101s)
- Rule 4: Trade front side of move, not back side; check MACD, look for huge rejection candles or volume tops to confirm front vs. back side. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @41:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=2492s)
- MACD filter rule: do not trade once MACD goes red; wait for MACD to flip positive before taking trade. — [`P5Sn_mWJdy0` @7:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=P5Sn_mWJdy0&t=442s)
- Must-have indicators: 9, 20, 200 exponential moving averages; volume weighted average price; MACD (one-minute only); volume bars. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @47:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2869s)
- Trick 9: Focus on trading only when MACD is positive; do not trade when MACD is negative. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @50:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=3026s)
- Keep charts simple: use only essential indicators (moving averages, VWAP, volume bars, MACD). — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @38:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=2335s)
- MACD should be traded only when positive/open; avoid trading after crossover into negative. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @41:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=2463s)
- MACD above signal line indicates favorable conditions; do not trade after MACD crosses below signal line. — [`_fsRWw0VbS4` @53:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_fsRWw0VbS4&t=3183s)
- Use technical indicators (9 EMA, 20 EMA, 200 EMA, VWAP, MACD) but keep charts uncluttered. — [`aAmmKTLbAts` @38:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aAmmKTLbAts&t=2287s)
- MACD condition: only trade when MACD is positive and above the signal line (front side of move). — [`iIC62xnblLc` @26:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iIC62xnblLc&t=1580s)
- Require MACD oscillator green (above signal line) before entering. — [`nH-z-089CgM` @5:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nH-z-089CgM&t=325s)
- Do not trade when MACD is negative; wait for crossover back to positive. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @9:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=552s)
- Do not trade the backside when MACD is negative. — [`vvXX2ycveuw` @9:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vvXX2ycveuw&t=552s)
- Use MACD crosses 20/9 EMA (faster crossover than alligator indicator). — [`w2owyBNunDQ` @36:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w2owyBNunDQ&t=2198s)

### VWAP (19)

- Use minimal indicators: focus on 9 EMA, 20 EMA, 200 EMA, VWAP, volume bars, and candlesticks. Avoid excessive technical indicators that cause analysis paralysis. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @6:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=374s)
- Add/scale aggressively as breakout confirms and stock pulls away from VWAP. — [`016ibIK58t0` @10:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=016ibIK58t0&t=629s)
- Do dip trades off moving averages and VWAP rather than breakout trades when a stock is rejecting prior highs. — [`9VAKgj1Px3s` @3:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VAKgj1Px3s&t=194s)
- VWAP is intraday indicator (does not work on daily charts); if stock is above VWAP it's bullish, below it's bearish; support/resistance crossovers signal control shift. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @65:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=3928s)
- If bands compressed and price below VWAP, break likely to downside (weakness signal). — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @14:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=871s)
- VWAP (volume-weighted average price): acts like "slack tide" (equilibrium); bulls in control if above VWAP, bears if below VWAP. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @42:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2535s)
- VWAP usage: place on all intraday charts (1-min, 5-min); intraday-only indicator, not for daily charts. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @19:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=1155s)
- VWAP interpretation: price above VWAP = bulls in control; price below VWAP = bears in control. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @25:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=1527s)
- Continuation trade: take break of VWAP setup on day two if stock is holding support and trending higher. — [`OnPTodRZl7o` @3:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OnPTodRZl7o&t=208s)
- Only trade the first swing back up from VWAP; after the element of surprise is gone and shorts are aware, subsequent attempts are less reliable. — [`SCy6RrASpJY` @27:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SCy6RrASpJY&t=1654s)
- VWAP definition: Calculation = (high+low+close)/3 × volume ÷ cumulative volume for the day. — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @4:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=252s)
- Use dojis with moving averages, VWAP, and volume weight to determine trade decision. — [`Ymzyd9fbsmI` @7:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ymzyd9fbsmI&t=468s)
- Hidden sellers at critical levels: sellers often stack large orders at chart resistance/VWAP to prevent breakout. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @8:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=484s)
- Trade only above volume weighted average price (VWAP). — [`nH-z-089CgM` @10:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nH-z-089CgM&t=636s)
- Bad price action checklist: no pattern resolution, topping tails, huge sellers on Level 2, nasty flushes/drops, unable to hold VWAP. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @10:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=613s)
- If below VWAP: trend down, sellers in control. If above VWAP: trend up, buyers in control. — [`qRirYfM4XiY` @2:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qRirYfM4XiY&t=170s)
- Price above VWAP indicates bullish control; below VWAP indicates bearish control (intraday equilibrium). — [`ul34Jfh-LOk` @18:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ul34Jfh-LOk&t=1097s)
- Use point of control with VWAP together: price between VWAP and POC = caution zone (mixed signals). Price above POC = strong bullish. Price below POC = weak bearish. — [`wC2Bbf6OMFQ` @16:12](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wC2Bbf6OMFQ&t=972s)
- Hold VWAP support: if stock drops to VWAP and bounces nicely, it confirms strength. — [`yBiAZvwpVww` @5:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yBiAZvwpVww&t=302s)

### support and resistance (15)

- 200 moving average on daily chart often acts as strong resistance/support. — [`BFH-0N8S-IA` @5:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BFH-0N8S-IA&t=332s)
- 9 EMA is a well-respected support level; when price comes down to 9 EMA with bottoming tail, additional confirmation to take trade. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @60:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=3613s)
- 9 EMA and 20 EMA: when price moves up fast, 9 EMA is closer to price than 20 EMA; if price breaks 9 EMA, check 20 EMA for next support. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @38:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=2320s)
- 20 EMA usage: second level of support after 9 EMA breaks. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @13:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=821s)
- 200 EMA (daily chart): acts as resistance when price is below it; acts as support when price is above it. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @15:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=938s)
- Pattern catches support around 9 EMA (exponential moving average). — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @12:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=720s)
- 200 exponential moving average (200 EMA): Major daily chart support/resistance level; check position before entering any trade. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @7:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=420s)
- Above 200 EMA = support; below 200 EMA = resistance. Price respects this level consistently. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @7:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=435s)
- Use 9 EMA and 20 EMA as support levels on intraday (1-minute, 5-minute) charts; 200 EMA on daily charts. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @20:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=1231s)
- 200-period moving average on daily chart acts as resistance when price is below it; acts as support when price is above it. — [`eTUYXkAr6Pc` @4:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eTUYXkAr6Pc&t=246s)
- Level 2 management: watch ask price, bid, and spread. Stacked ask (10,000+ share sizes) = resistance. Large bid order = support. — [`iBNcF_lPBIg` @38:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iBNcF_lPBIg&t=2332s)
- Read Level 2: large buyers at support levels indicate that level will hold; when that buyer is absorbed by sellers, expect sharp drops. — [`pJuG5YtVF84` @5:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pJuG5YtVF84&t=331s)
- Watch for large stacked orders on level 2 (e.g., 20,000-share seller) as signals of resistance/support; when consumed, expect a move. — [`pJuG5YtVF84` @14:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pJuG5YtVF84&t=872s)
- Step 3: Analyze the daily chart; look for nearby resistance and distance to 200 moving average. — [`sv6U1nAO8BI` @15:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sv6U1nAO8BI&t=918s)
- The 200 period moving average on daily chart (average price over 200 days) acts as support when price is above it and resistance when price is below it. — [`w2owyBNunDQ` @6:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w2owyBNunDQ&t=419s)

### level 2 / time and sales (14)

- Execute trades on Level 2, not just charts. Depth-of-market data is more important than chart patterns alone. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @8:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=497s)
- Level 2 data subscription: Weeble charges $2.99/month for Level 2, $9.99/month for NBBO (total ~$12/month). — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @34:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=2084s)
- Level 2 observation: if bid/ask is static (e.g., $3.50 ask not moving despite buying), indicates iceberg order (hidden large seller). — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @7:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=447s)
- Sentiment shift indicator: Watch tape for change from red orders (selling) to green orders (buying); this is buyable moment. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @15:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=955s)
- Trade simulator extensively to gain experience reading level 2 and order book. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @26:19](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=1579s)
- Focus eye on the ask (offer) side of Level 2 when waiting for price to move higher. — [`_9za7jNDPQc` @32:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_9za7jNDPQc&t=1974s)
- Use Level 2 to verify order liquidity before attempting large sells (e.g., check if buyer exists before selling 10,000 shares). — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @5:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=323s)
- Time and sales tape reading: green orders = buying at ask (strength); red orders = selling at bid (weakness); white = between spread. — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @26:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=1594s)
- Only use Level 2 on active stocks; Level 2 on low-volume stocks is pointless. — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @24:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=1458s)
- Use Level 2 to execute trades and manage open positions; keep charts on separate monitor to understand context. — [`eY7JdVjg0kw` @2:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eY7JdVjg0kw&t=124s)
- On level 2, look at the bid-ask spread width; prefer stocks with tight spreads (1-3 cents) for better profit potential. — [`pJuG5YtVF84` @8:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pJuG5YtVF84&t=482s)
- Step 4: Analyze level 2 depth-of-market; check spread thickness and order sizes. — [`sv6U1nAO8BI` @17:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sv6U1nAO8BI&t=1033s)
- Use a broker with reliable platform, fast order execution, full Level 2 market data, hot keys for rapid execution, and simulated trading capability. — [`w97KlUrVDk0` @18:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w97KlUrVDk0&t=1126s)
- Identify 5-cent tick stocks by looking at Level 2: quotes only at nickels and dimes. — [`xCsTmqzBIco` @28:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xCsTmqzBIco&t=1711s)

### moving average (9 EMA) (5)

- 9 EMA is the fastest; 20 is slower; 200 is the slowest—use all three on all timeframes. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @6:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=374s)
- Moving averages to display: 9 EMA, 20 EMA, and 200 EMA on all timeframes (1-min, 5-min, daily). — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @14:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=857s)
- Use 9 EMA, 20 EMA, and 200 MA as core technical indicators on all timeframes. — [`YiMItz4fgFk` @42:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YiMItz4fgFk&t=2567s)
- Use 9 EMA, 20 EMA, 200 EMA on all timeframes as foundation indicators. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @40:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=2401s)
- Apply 9 EMA, 20 EMA, 200 EMA on one-minute, five-minute, and daily charts. — [`qRirYfM4XiY` @5:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qRirYfM4XiY&t=315s)

### daily chart context (2)

- Reverse split indicator of potential big bounce; check daily chart for recent reverse split notation. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @1:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=73s)
- The 200-period exponential moving average is one of the most respected levels on daily charts. — [`YZ5nvP7DnQQ` @14:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YZ5nvP7DnQQ&t=872s)

### uncategorised (15)

- Use 9 exponential moving average on all timeframes (1-minute, 5-minute, daily). — [`4syXgXshgsc` @37:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=2277s)
- Compressed Bollinger Bands → often leads to expansion breakout (narrow range break). — [`DgWn3egDGb0` @13:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=DgWn3egDGb0&t=788s)
- Keep charts simple; use only moving averages and candlestick patterns; avoid indicator clutter. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @37:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=2265s)
- Use exponential moving averages (EMA), not simple moving averages (SMA), for faster response to price. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @14:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=857s)
- Bollinger bands settings: 20-period moving average with 2 standard deviations. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @50:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=3015s)
- 99% of price action occurs within Bollinger bands; price outside bands is extreme. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @51:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=3100s)
- When all three moving average lines are twisted/intertwined together, the alligator is sleeping and the market is in consolidation; avoid trading during this phase. — [`NE0IxWksuqo` @3:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NE0IxWksuqo&t=219s)
- Do not allow stock to break below moving average; that is a no-go. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @12:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=768s)
- When a moving average breaks and holds in the opposite direction, that signals a real trend shift worth trading for a longer-term move. — [`aC7jCwYYHxY` @14:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aC7jCwYYHxY&t=892s)
- Consistent indicator usage: must apply technical indicator or system consistently; using it only 10% of the time means missing its signal 90% of the time. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @28:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1713s)
- If price breaks below the 200 EMA and holds below it, often signals the end of the uptrend; this is a sign of weakness. — [`eTUYXkAr6Pc` @20:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eTUYXkAr6Pc&t=1231s)
- Price above 200-day EMA: prefer stocks trending above this level. — [`mgUoZPlGGas` @39:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mgUoZPlGGas&t=2365s)
- Use exponential moving averages (EMA) for faster line curvature than simple MA. — [`qRirYfM4XiY` @2:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qRirYfM4XiY&t=172s)
- Do not overlay 20 EMA on one-minute chart just because it works elsewhere; understand timeframe nesting. — [`qRirYfM4XiY` @7:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qRirYfM4XiY&t=430s)
- Price above moving average is bullish; price below moving average is bearish. — [`w2owyBNunDQ` @4:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w2owyBNunDQ&t=243s)

## psychology (17 rules)

### emotional control (15)

- Tag all FOMO-driven trades with the "FOMO" label for tracking and accountability. — [`2higxJXgl6o` @6:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2higxJXgl6o&t=360s)
- Move profits out of trading account periodically to enforce discipline and prevent large-size emotional trading. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @19:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=1150s)
- Presence of mind technique: vocalize emotion ("I'm feeling angry/afraid") to gain awareness and control response. — [`8eLtork_M50` @41:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8eLtork_M50&t=2502s)
- RULE 3: Delete mobile app when emotionally hijacked to avoid checking stock prices while experiencing FOMO. — [`H9BRF4xynQA` @10:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=H9BRF4xynQA&t=651s)
- Wait patiently for known strengths rather than chasing other traders' strategies. — [`M07xElrhVYM` @57:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=3434s)
- Stick to your trading plan with discipline. — [`N3E5eYS8d-I` @5:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=N3E5eYS8d-I&t=331s)
- Once you're done trading, do not check charts for rest of day due to FOMO risk. — [`anuxv488waA` @17:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=anuxv488waA&t=1028s)
- Walking away before losing 50% of daily profit is key discipline. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @25:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=1516s)
- Identify personal emotional triggers (FOMO, frustration, anger). — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @24:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=1454s) _(2 videos)_
- Practice meditation and exercise regularly to build emotional discipline. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @28:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=1739s)
- Do not trade into close/after hours if already up for day (FOMO guard). — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @7:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=420s)
- Practice meditation and consistent exercise to strengthen emotional discipline. — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @24:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=1482s)
- Discipline and consistent rule-following are essential; without this, consistently making $10 per day is impossible. — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @2:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=161s)
- Recovery rule 3: practice mindfulness/meditation daily (guides for specific emotions: anger, anxiety, FOMO, discipline, patience). — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @34:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=2067s)
- Keep a trading discipline during rough patches rather than taking a break completely; get back on the horse immediately after losses. — [`xauLV_WHXgc` @4:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xauLV_WHXgc&t=248s)

### uncategorised (2)

- Trading mindset = 50% psychology, 50% skill/strategy. — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @2:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=124s)
- Primary goal: produce one week of stable trading results to rebuild confidence. — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @38:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=2283s)

## account-admin (82 rules)

### paper trading / simulator (27)

- Step 2: Practice in trading simulator before using real money. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @6:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=363s)
- Warrior Trading Simulator offers instant order execution for rapid trading practice. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @12:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=723s)
- Weeble simulator is slow; Thinker Swim simulator limited to regular hours only. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @14:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=861s)
- Broker platform transition: practice on Weeble simulator, trade live with Weeble; practice Thinker Swim simulator, trade live with one share in Thinker Swim. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @40:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2400s)
- Practice with simulator before risking real money; always manage risk first, profit second. — [`5AV2OLWD1gc` @31:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5AV2OLWD1gc&t=1882s)
- Thinkorswim simulator: Does NOT support extended hours trading. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=418s)
- Weeble simulator: DOES support extended hours trading. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=362s)
- Advanced hotkeys unavailable on Thinkorswim for both simulator AND production real money accounts. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=377s)
- Weeble: Offers excellent hotkeys BUT only with real money production version (not in simulator). — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=394s)
- Warrior Trading Sim: Offers advanced hotkeys in simulator. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=409s)
- Resumption quotes: Not available Thinkorswim simulator; available on Weeble (funded) and Warrior Trading Sim. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @7:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=421s)
- Simulator vs. production execution speed: Brokers allocate MORE server resources to real money (production) than simulators. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @12:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=728s)
- Paper trading position tracking issue at Weeble: Simulator doesn't keep open positions; real money does. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @35:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=2105s)
- Do not trade with real money until proven profitable in simulator first. — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @9:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=575s)
- Simulate trading before using real money to gain platform familiarity and prove strategy viability. — [`Bqo2XGDZtVU` @12:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Bqo2XGDZtVU&t=722s)
- Simulate before live trading; prove profitability in sim, then start small live. — [`QBXlHOCIl8U` @50:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QBXlHOCIl8U&t=3023s)
- Begin with simulator trading to build pattern recognition. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @26:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=1561s)
- Practice in a simulator first with real-time data before trading live capital. — [`dl1MtlsMoZw` @13:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dl1MtlsMoZw&t=789s)
- Step 2: Practice the strategy in a simulated trading environment for a few months. — [`fJcWMZ0YZGY` @13:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fJcWMZ0YZGY&t=795s)
- Step 3: Switch to real money trading as soon as you've proven proficiency in the simulator. — [`fJcWMZ0YZGY` @23:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fJcWMZ0YZGY&t=1380s)
- Do not spend extended time (years) practicing in simulators; transition to real money after proving skill. — [`fJcWMZ0YZGY` @24:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fJcWMZ0YZGY&t=1483s)
- Paper trading phase: Start with paper trading (simulator) for months before attempting real money to build consistency. — [`i1shm3ATwJI` @10:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=i1shm3ATwJI&t=651s)
- Never trade with real money until reaching multiple weeks (5-6 weeks) of consistent profitability in simulator with good metrics. — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @27:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=1662s)
- First draw-down in simulator is critical psychological milestone; recover from it before trading real money. — [`m7pV69LTYDw` @28:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m7pV69LTYDw&t=1681s)
- Before trading real money, prove profitability in simulator: at least 3-4 weeks of green trading, good profit/loss ratio, and consistency. — [`taHYs-v3hBQ` @5:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=taHYs-v3hBQ&t=333s)
- Do not open a real brokerage account until you have proven consistent profitability in simulator. — [`taHYs-v3hBQ` @6:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=taHYs-v3hBQ&t=387s)
- Practice all new strategies in a simulator before putting real money at risk. — [`w97KlUrVDk0` @8:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w97KlUrVDk0&t=481s)

### PDT rule (17)

- PDT rule: any trader with margin account executing more than three day trades within five business days must maintain minimum $25,000 account balance. — [`ryOcGLJNOJU` @0:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ryOcGLJNOJU&t=21s) _(2 videos)_
- If you hit PDT violation on margin account under $25,000, broker will demand deposit or lock account; you can call broker to "reset" the violation once. — [`OLdOfU01wHY` @26:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLdOfU01wHY&t=1588s)
- PDT rule defined: taking more than three day trades in a 5-day period triggers pattern day trader classification. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @0:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=15s)
- Under PDT rule: must maintain $25,000 minimum account balance to day trade using margin. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @0:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=27s)
- With $25,000 margin account: get 4x leverage automatically. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @0:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=44s)
- Proposed change: FINRA approved lowering PDT minimum from $25,000 to $2,000. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @1:28](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=88s)
- With $2,000 PDT minimum: settlement margin alone allows unlimited daily trading without leverage. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @12:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=752s)
- Brokers could impose their own restrictions: e.g., require $10,000+ for 4x leverage even if PDT allows $2,000. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @12:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=758s)
- Small account challenge: funded with $2,000 to demonstrate new PDT rule environment. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @16:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=964s)
- To avoid PDT: use cash account where cash must settle before next trade (no unsettled cash trading allowed). — [`ryOcGLJNOJU` @10:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ryOcGLJNOJU&t=632s)
- Once account grows above $25,000, convert from cash to margin account and PDT no longer restricts trading. — [`ryOcGLJNOJU` @10:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ryOcGLJNOJU&t=658s)
- US brokers (Thinkorswim, Robin Hood, Lightspeed, Webull) MUST enforce PDT for US residents. — [`ryOcGLJNOJU` @16:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ryOcGLJNOJU&t=969s)
- Non-US brokers (Interactive Brokers international, Webull international, TradeZero) do NOT enforce PDT on non-US residents. — [`ryOcGLJNOJU` @17:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ryOcGLJNOJU&t=1070s)
- Minimum account: $500 (Sure Trader broker, does not enforce PDT rule). — [`xTPcI7HHu5w` @6:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xTPcI7HHu5w&t=378s)
- If you hold overnight, PDT rule doesn't apply (swing trading exemption). — [`y_Qp_ev2js8` @1:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y_Qp_ev2js8&t=80s)
- On margin account, execute more than three trades in a five-day period = flagged as PDT. — [`y_Qp_ev2js8` @5:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y_Qp_ev2js8&t=355s)
- PDT-flagged accounts must maintain minimum $25,000 balance. — [`y_Qp_ev2js8` @6:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y_Qp_ev2js8&t=410s)

### hotkeys (3)

- Offset hotkey strategy: Create limit orders 10 cents above the ask (offset) to increase fill probability on fast-moving stocks. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @24:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=1487s)
- Use sticky notes on keyboard labeled with what each hotkey does to build muscle memory. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @9:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=549s)
- Look for brokers with fast execution tools (hotkeys, direct access) not commission-free brokers designed for casual investors. — [`taHYs-v3hBQ` @6:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=taHYs-v3hBQ&t=413s)

### uncategorised (35)

- Warrior Trading Sim users: transition to Sterling Trader Pro licensed broker (Lightspeed, Guardian, Centerpoint). — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @40:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2423s)
- Broker commission comparison: Ross pays more than $175/month in commissions vs. monthly software fees at some brokers. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @8:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=490s)
- Do not invest on margin/leverage as inexperienced investor; use diversified approach instead. — [`ICbPz3iXbG0` @25:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ICbPz3iXbG0&t=1555s)
- To short a stock: must have margin account; margin agreement required because you are borrowing stock. — [`OLdOfU01wHY` @36:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLdOfU01wHY&t=2162s)
- After hours trading does not allow leverage; must liquidate all margin positions by 4:00pm ET or broker auto-liquidates. — [`PjVivCcM1B0` @26:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=PjVivCcM1B0&t=1592s)
- On small accounts, start with commission-free brokers; graduate to direct-access brokers like Lightspeed only after proving consistency. — [`Q-7eBHjD1ks` @62:36](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Q-7eBHjD1ks&t=3756s)
- Puerto Rico residents (live there 6 months and 1 day per year) pay zero federal income tax on trading profits. — [`SWPCn0ZDPU0` @1:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SWPCn0ZDPU0&t=108s)
- Backdoor Roth conversion: contribute $6,000 to traditional IRA (pre-tax), immediately convert to Roth IRA and pay tax on conversion amount only. — [`SWPCn0ZDPU0` @6:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SWPCn0ZDPU0&t=363s)
- Mark-to-market election must be filed by April 15th of the CURRENT tax year for that year to apply (cannot be done retroactively for prior years). — [`SWPCn0ZDPU0` @9:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SWPCn0ZDPU0&t=593s)
- Shorting requires a margin account; cash accounts cannot short. — [`TTvaXCmtVbI` @3:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TTvaXCmtVbI&t=181s)
- Cover position before days-to-cover expiration; broker will auto-liquidate after that deadline. — [`TTvaXCmtVbI` @8:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=TTvaXCmtVbI&t=525s)
- Leverage = 4x account balance automatically with margin account. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @1:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=107s)
- Margin loan: broker extends credit for one day so trader can trade multiple times before settlement. — [`Xy3SeJcUYew` @9:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Xy3SeJcUYew&t=587s)
- Direct routing preferred over smart routing to avoid broker middleman and payment-for-orderflow issues. — [`a2xlibTeVO4` @17:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=a2xlibTeVO4&t=1073s)
- When creating an LLC, make an S-Corp election (Es-Corp) to avoid double taxation. — [`bUAYYcgvvy8` @6:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bUAYYcgvvy8&t=363s)
- Open broker account in the business name using the company's EIN. — [`bUAYYcgvvy8` @6:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bUAYYcgvvy8&t=378s)
- To be eligible for Trader Tax Status, must seek to profit from daily market movements (easy). — [`bUAYYcgvvy8` @9:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bUAYYcgvvy8&t=547s)
- After filing Mark-to-Market election, notify broker to update accounting method. — [`bUAYYcgvvy8` @16:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=bUAYYcgvvy8&t=971s)
- One trade per day maximum (limited by cash settlement overnight; no margin). — [`d1CRtDuUARQ` @11:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=d1CRtDuUARQ&t=660s)
- Use 6x leverage account (international broker) to convert $1,000 into $6,000 buying power. Leverage limited to stocks above $5 per share. — [`iBNcF_lPBIg` @4:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iBNcF_lPBIg&t=271s)
- Gross margin: must be positive. — [`mgUoZPlGGas` @36:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mgUoZPlGGas&t=2194s)
- Retirement account strategy: use Roth IRA for tax-free growth and dividends. — [`mgUoZPlGGas` @43:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mgUoZPlGGas&t=2634s)
- Contribute to tax-free accounts (IRA, Roth IRA, 401k) regardless of employment type. — [`nDMX11XmYF0` @16:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nDMX11XmYF0&t=962s)
- Prioritize building trading capital in tax-free accounts over taxable accounts. — [`nDMX11XmYF0` @19:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nDMX11XmYF0&t=1197s)
- Borrowing fee charged daily by broker; fee varies by stock availability. — [`qBfCXK_euDA` @3:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qBfCXK_euDA&t=181s)
- Must cover position within days-to-cover limit (7-30 days) or broker will auto-liquidate. — [`qBfCXK_euDA` @3:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qBfCXK_euDA&t=188s)
- Always set hard stops on short positions; losses can exceed account balance requiring margin call. — [`qBfCXK_euDA` @18:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=qBfCXK_euDA&t=1091s)
- On large losses, respond with: (1) acknowledge heightened emotional state, (2) apply account restrictions (cap max shares via broker), (3) practice mindfulness/meditation, (4) trade small size for base hits only, (5) do not track P&L. — [`s7kEHvd0TJk` @17:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=s7kEHvd0TJk&t=1045s)
- Minimum deposit requirement: $500 (started with $542, meaning $42.22 margin above minimum). — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @2:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=126s)
- Commission-free brokers use payment for order flow (send order to market maker, not exchange) so you don't get rebates. — [`tdjOKh5Jf9w` @13:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tdjOKh5Jf9w&t=815s)
- _...5 more._

## other (527 rules)

### support and resistance (42)

- Warrior Trading Sim: DOES support extended hours trading. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=364s)
- Psychological level resistance: stocks respect half-dollar and whole-dollar marks ($3.50, $4.00, $4.50, $5.00). — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @6:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=390s)
- Resistance often forms at psychological whole dollar and half-dollar levels. — [`BFH-0N8S-IA` @15:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BFH-0N8S-IA&t=941s)
- Support and resistance clustered around half-dollars and whole-dollar levels due to order book concentration; test these levels multiple times before breaking through. — [`BUCPPCXOHbs` @69:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=BUCPPCXOHbs&t=4158s)
- When stock breaks below support channel, consider trade over; avoid chasing. — [`Fe4BaF8gCOQ` @5:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Fe4BaF8gCOQ&t=356s)
- Stocks trade with respect to psychological support/resistance at whole dollars and half dollars. — [`Gf791LDEsQI` @1:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Gf791LDEsQI&t=70s)
- Identify resistance levels on the daily chart as targets (in this case, $12.47, then $15.75). — [`IKwm-KdCqpg` @3:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IKwm-KdCqpg&t=182s)
- Scale into positions: add at ascending resistance levels ($10.28, $10.40, $10.50, $10.75) as price moves higher. — [`IKwm-KdCqpg` @3:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IKwm-KdCqpg&t=225s)
- Volume profile point of control: where most volume traded; act as support when price above, resistance when below. — [`LNDd7rf-9FU` @58:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=LNDd7rf-9FU&t=3497s)
- Order spoofing: placing orders with no intention of them being filled, only to influence other traders' perception of support/resistance; illegal practice. — [`OLdOfU01wHY` @50:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLdOfU01wHY&t=3010s)
- A big block of sellers on the offer prevents breakouts and creates resistance that leads to false breakouts. — [`OQLfHuO8KlI` @7:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OQLfHuO8KlI&t=449s)
- Add support at 9.40, then 9.50, then 9.75, with full 30,000-share position targeting break of $10.00. — [`RUuJzmz5jCg` @6:53](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RUuJzmz5jCg&t=413s)
- If stock is consolidating at support/resistance: wait for break of critical level before entering. — [`SpnidshD8Zk` @12:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SpnidshD8Zk&t=755s)
- Hold at support levels; if price holds above half-dollar (e.g., $4.50), higher probability to break upside. — [`SpnidshD8Zk` @14:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SpnidshD8Zk&t=871s)
- On squeeze moves, add into dips and take half profits at key resistance levels. — [`Uft2BuJpap8` @3:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Uft2BuJpap8&t=211s)
- When stock breaks through resistance, add back and take more profit into next resistance level. — [`Uft2BuJpap8` @3:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Uft2BuJpap8&t=221s)
- Half-dollar and whole-dollar price levels: Most financial instruments show resistance at $X.50 and $X.00 price points. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @8:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=526s)
- Intraday resistance progression example: $6.50 → $7.00 → $7.50 → $8.00 → $8.50 → $9.00 (step-by-step resistance). — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @9:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=550s)
- Double top pattern: Two attempts to break resistance at same level; typically rejects on second/third attempt; avoid buying here. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @13:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=812s)
- Profit-taking rule: Take partial profits as price approaches resistance levels; don't be greedy. — [`WQi0KvdLnwM` @22:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WQi0KvdLnwM&t=1355s)
- Add for break of resistance high once pattern completes. — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @18:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=1125s)
- Only look for next level of resistance by looking to the left and up (previous highs that haven't been broken yet). — [`YZ5nvP7DnQQ` @5:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YZ5nvP7DnQQ&t=356s)
- Draw support/resistance at half dollars and whole dollars where psychological resistance congregates. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @23:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=1383s)
- When price breaks resistance, expect a retest; if it holds above, that level becomes new support. — [`ZpiWEMTpvoo` @30:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ZpiWEMTpvoo&t=1832s)
- Use 9 and 20 exponential moving averages to identify levels of support during pullbacks. — [`_fsRWw0VbS4` @50:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_fsRWw0VbS4&t=3054s)
- Cup and handle: requires double top resistance; trade the break through that double top on the handle. — [`aqTXoV923OE` @68:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aqTXoV923OE&t=4118s)
- Consolidation before breakout: tighter consolidation (support and resistance closer) = more likely false breakout; coiling pattern (support getting closer together) = more conviction before break. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @21:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1260s)
- Large sellers on ask are resistance; until bought up, stock won't move higher. — [`eY7JdVjg0kw` @14:14](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eY7JdVjg0kw&t=854s)
- Price typically breaks through levels quickly once one level is broken, moving to next psychological resistance level. — [`hUhFuaxAsiw` @6:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hUhFuaxAsiw&t=375s)
- Horizontal trend lines at half dollars and whole dollars represent psychological resistance from traders placing profit targets. — [`hUhFuaxAsiw` @10:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hUhFuaxAsiw&t=620s)
- _...12 more._

### volume confirmation (30)

- Recommended trading window: 7:00am-10:00am ET (when most brokers come online; highest volume). — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @5:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=300s)
- Focus on less trading (fewer trades) yields better results than high volume trading. September (102 trades) was best month; January and December (high trade count) underperformed. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @31:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=1905s)
- Disregard stocks with less than 25 million shares of volume despite high percentage gains. — [`6Yszkb7ZZoY` @33:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6Yszkb7ZZoY&t=1984s)
- Use level two to gauge sentiment and volume strength; avoid trading when heavy selling is evident. — [`6w-7geIsq1A` @5:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6w-7geIsq1A&t=327s)
- Do not overstay the trade; step off when the move shows signs of exhaustion or volume selling increases. — [`6w-7geIsq1A` @14:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6w-7geIsq1A&t=875s)
- Customize active trader window: remove unused columns (expiration, volume) to reduce clutter. — [`C4XZ_67c0Fo` @16:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=C4XZ_67c0Fo&t=986s)
- Portfolio rule: only trade stocks currently making new highs and having highest volume in market (follow the flow). — [`EHnIFKnJiXA` @39:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EHnIFKnJiXA&t=2388s)
- On slow volume days, accept lower expectations: if leading gainers are only up 30%, expect a slow day. — [`IKwm-KdCqpg` @2:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IKwm-KdCqpg&t=142s)
- If a stock has heavy options volume and open interest, there will be greater battles at key price levels around expiration. — [`MMwiCswgwHU` @18:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MMwiCswgwHU&t=1126s)
- Confirm green on tape (buying volume) before entering; do not get in during continued red selling. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @17:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=1074s)
- Day-two characteristics: volume typically lower, price action choppier, element of surprise gone; fewer predictable patterns. — [`OnPTodRZl7o` @2:18](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OnPTodRZl7o&t=138s)
- Route orders through NASDAQ for optimal order execution speed, as it receives the most volume. — [`Q-7eBHjD1ks` @16:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Q-7eBHjD1ks&t=962s)
- Do not want to see stock pull back below the volume weighted average price. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @19:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=1156s)
- Mid-day: 11:00am-3:00pm; volume dies down, not worth trading. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @34:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2046s)
- Best window: roughly 7:00am-11:00am for trading with most volume and reliability. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @35:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2130s)
- Trick 10: Read the volume story; high volume buying candles + low volume selling candles = bullish. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @52:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=3165s)
- Level one data includes: bid, offer, last price, volume today. — [`SrYGENemM1w` @1:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SrYGENemM1w&t=115s)
- Use direct routing to high-volume routes (NASDAQ) rather than obscure routes to reduce rerouting. — [`SrYGENemM1w` @12:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SrYGENemM1w&t=766s)
- Continuation setup defined: stock strong on day one (30–100%+ move), then day two and three "keep creeping higher" on reduced volume. — [`_RRClNoHamw` @0:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_RRClNoHamw&t=11s)
- Volume confirmation at breakout: if breakout happens on light volume, odds are high it reverses; need more volume coming in at the breakout to confirm. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @9:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=590s)
- Multi-timeframe alignment check: stock extended up? Consolidation on 1-minute with declining volume = higher probability pop-and-fade; consolidation with volume increasing = more likely resolves up. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @12:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=755s)
- Volume profile as primary: prioritize high-volume candles on breakout; that is biggest sign of conviction. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @28:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1721s)
- Market open first 5 minutes: most volatile, highest volume period; book $400-700 profit, then use as cushion for rest of day. — [`eCSzHYl8apo` @1:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=eCSzHYl8apo&t=92s)
- Only trade stocks with five times higher volume than the 50-day average volume. — [`m5zu_X-_51I` @15:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m5zu_X-_51I&t=902s)
- Average daily volume minimum: over 1 million shares. — [`mgUoZPlGGas` @36:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=mgUoZPlGGas&t=2160s)
- Breakouts and consolidations should resolve cleanly; if multiple rejections occur on high volume, price action is poor. — [`nfCqhdfyLKo` @3:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nfCqhdfyLKo&t=203s)
- Never trade stocks dominated by high-frequency trading (60% algorithmic volume); focus on retail-heavy stocks instead. — [`txWaMpSzHhM` @15:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=txWaMpSzHhM&t=935s)
- Volume profile setting: use "visible range" mode with standard settings (value area 70%, row size 24, volume up vs. down). — [`wC2Bbf6OMFQ` @6:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wC2Bbf6OMFQ&t=360s)
- Use volume profile on 5-minute chart, NOT 1-minute (1-minute zoom level makes profile inaccurate). Keep 5-min chart zoomed out. — [`wC2Bbf6OMFQ` @7:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=wC2Bbf6OMFQ&t=421s)
- Stock price must be under $20 to attract sufficient retail volume for large moves. — [`xgnqOu_fchA` @20:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xgnqOu_fchA&t=1245s)

### halts (24)

- Circuit breaker halts: stock above $3 halts on 10% move in 5 minutes; below $3 halts on 20% move in 5 minutes. — [`0Zh105lq9Rk` @10:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=0Zh105lq9Rk&t=603s)
- Halt levels: Thinkorswim does NOT show them; Weeble shows them (funded account only); Warrior Trading Sim shows them. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @6:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=412s)
- Thinkorswim with Dastrader addon (third-party): Costs $150-$175/month to add hotkeys, halt levels, resumption quotes. — [`5aWoZdbXJrA` @9:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5aWoZdbXJrA&t=548s)
- Volatility halt (LULD or circuit breaker halt) lasts exactly 5 minutes minimum. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @1:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=84s)
- T12 halt = SEC/exchange suspended trading due to investigation or unusual activity request; can last weeks/months. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @2:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=126s)
- Halt bands prevent stock from moving more than certain % in less than 5 minutes. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @5:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=301s)
- Tier 1 securities (S&P 500 companies) above $3: halt if move more than 5% in less than 5 minutes (up or down). — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @6:25](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=385s)
- Tier 2 securities (most small-caps) above $0.75 with previous close above $3: halt bands are 10% from reference price. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @9:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=540s)
- Tier 2 securities (small-caps) with previous close $0.75-$3: halt bands are 20% (twice as wide as tier above $3). — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @9:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=595s)
- Tier 3 securities below $0.75: halt every 15 cents OR 75%, whichever is lesser. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @10:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=624s)
- If stock reaches halt level and stays there 15 seconds, halt triggers. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @13:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=795s)
- No orders can be placed above/below halt band (limit order restriction). — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @14:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=875s)
- Stock halted going up typically resumes higher; halted going down typically resumes lower. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @19:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=1143s)
- Avoid trading restricted stocks (marked as not-tradeable at brokers) or those with poor liquidity that halt frequently. — [`IKwm-KdCqpg` @5:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IKwm-KdCqpg&t=344s)
- Focus on three core setups Danny excels at: (1) parabolic squeezing action, (2) halts and false halts, (3) readable price action "base hits." — [`M07xElrhVYM` @56:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=M07xElrhVYM&t=3383s)
- Tier one securities (S&P 500 companies): halt if move more than 5% within 5 minute period; only 9:30am-4:00pm. — [`OLdOfU01wHY` @44:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLdOfU01wHY&t=2646s)
- Tier two securities (other stocks) above $3: halt if move more than 10% within 5 minute period; only 9:30am-4:00pm. — [`OLdOfU01wHY` @44:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLdOfU01wHY&t=2674s)
- Pre- and post-market halts: no circuit breaker halts from 4:00am-9:30am or 4:00pm-8:00pm; stock can move 900-1000% without halting. — [`OLdOfU01wHY` @47:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OLdOfU01wHY&t=2852s)
- Rule 3: Avoid halt risk; check halt levels. — [`ORWJzImSTdE` @31:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ORWJzImSTdE&t=1910s)
- When a stock breaks its volatility band and holds outside for 15 seconds, it triggers a 5-minute halt. — [`StpXbe3Ga3Y` @18:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=StpXbe3Ga3Y&t=1111s)
- When stocks halt going down, they usually open lower when resumed (due to lack of buyers); when halting going up, they usually open higher (shorts covering and momentum continuing). — [`StpXbe3Ga3Y` @26:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=StpXbe3Ga3Y&t=1607s)
- When trading out of halts, add over the high of the halt if stock opens lower, or add at whole dollar levels (e.g., $4) if stock opens higher. — [`StpXbe3Ga3Y` @28:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=StpXbe3Ga3Y&t=1685s)
- After-halt: Shorts scramble to cover during resumption, often dips then rips higher. — [`W3jXQlgGbBc` @10:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W3jXQlgGbBc&t=623s)
- Hold small size on resumption: after halt, held small size and sold as price came back down. — [`yBiAZvwpVww` @8:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yBiAZvwpVww&t=482s)

### accuracy / win rate (20)

- Track accuracy, average winners, average losers, biggest winner, biggest loser, profitability by day/time/price range. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @26:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1590s)
- Phase 2 proof of concept test: take one trade per day for 10 days; goal is 60%+ accuracy (6+ winners). — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @38:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2288s)
- Step 6 (Phase 3 part one): build track record with 10 days of one-trade-per-day; goal 60%+ accuracy + profitability. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @44:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2647s)
- A-quality setups in cold market: ~68% accuracy (vs 80%+ in hot market). — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @16:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=998s)
- B-quality setups in cold market: ~50-53% accuracy. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @16:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=1016s)
- C-quality setups in cold market: ~40% accuracy (should avoid). — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @17:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=1021s)
- Only trade A-quality in cold markets to maintain profitability; sacrifice frequency for accuracy. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @18:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=1082s)
- Risk $2 to make $1 requires 66% accuracy just to break-even (unfavorable). — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @14:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=841s)
- On 1-to-1 ratio, break-even is 50% accuracy; on 2-to-1 ratio, break-even is 33% accuracy. — [`EzHEGVb_9-c` @26:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=EzHEGVb_9-c&t=1562s)
- Statistically: With 15-20 trades per day at 65-70% accuracy, approximately 10-11 winners expected daily, allowing green days nearly always possible if staying disciplined. — [`HkCzaGuSJ1g` @3:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HkCzaGuSJ1g&t=200s)
- Minimum accuracy requirement in phase one: 60% (6 out of 10 trades profitable). — [`OHe6_FpxO0g` @28:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=OHe6_FpxO0g&t=1726s)
- Accuracy (win rate) is approximately 69%. — [`W6BnX1T3KZ0` @11:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=W6BnX1T3KZ0&t=698s)
- Aim for 75% accuracy through consistency and rule-following during a small account challenge (targeting $25,000 goal). — [`YiMItz4fgFk` @1:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=YiMItz4fgFk&t=118s)
- Risk should be $1 to make $2 (2-to-1 profit-to-loss ratio); aim for 65% accuracy with actual outcomes often closer to 1-to-1. — [`js25lIZMUSY` @31:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=js25lIZMUSY&t=1869s)
- Maintain detailed trading log of all trades to track metrics: profit/loss ratio, win rate, consistency by day/week/month. — [`taHYs-v3hBQ` @14:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=taHYs-v3hBQ&t=842s)
- Risk to reward ratio baseline: if risking $1 to make $1, need 50% accuracy to break even. — [`vPMyjSCgf58` @10:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vPMyjSCgf58&t=643s)
- Risk to reward ratio: if risking $1 to make $2, only need 33% accuracy to be profitable. — [`vPMyjSCgf58` @11:08](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=vPMyjSCgf58&t=668s)
- Profit trifecta: (1) Profit/loss ratio, (2) Accuracy (%, (3) Consistency (weeks of green). — [`xTPcI7HHu5w` @30:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xTPcI7HHu5w&t=1830s)
- At least 65-70% accuracy is baseline for profitability. — [`xTPcI7HHu5w` @31:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xTPcI7HHu5w&t=1870s)
- One-to-one profit/loss ratio: risk $1 to make $1; if accuracy >50%, breakeven; with 68% accuracy, highly profitable. — [`xTPcI7HHu5w` @31:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xTPcI7HHu5w&t=1873s)

### small cap focus (10)

- Penny stocks (by US regulator definition) are stocks trading below $3 per share, though literal pennies are below $1. — [`1JOg8wZoeBI` @6:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1JOg8wZoeBI&t=362s)
- Do not trade if spread is too wide (e.g., over $1 on penny stock indicates poor liquidity). — [`4syXgXshgsc` @32:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4syXgXshgsc&t=1962s)
- Do not trade NYSE/AMEX small caps expecting to see resumption price like NASDAQ provides. — [`FN-uqfbEVKw` @26:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FN-uqfbEVKw&t=1575s)
- Don't trade penny stocks under $1; commissions are per-share so cost is higher. — [`HY7i78bOJFA` @8:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HY7i78bOJFA&t=481s)
- Do not short small-cap stocks due to unlimited loss risk. — [`N3iqOuF8wQU` @4:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=N3iqOuF8wQU&t=247s)
- Do not trade penny stocks below $1 per share. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @8:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=481s)
- Avoid penny stocks (sub-$1) as they lack the stability needed for consistent trading. — [`_NAM_oTYUQM` @18:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_NAM_oTYUQM&t=1114s)
- Avoid jumping between strategies when one isn't working (small caps → penny stocks → options). — [`_O0AEVPar7k` @15:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_O0AEVPar7k&t=913s)
- Trade only penny stocks listed on NASDAQ or NYSE (not OTC); minimum 10 cents per share preferred to reduce risk. — [`pYx756KSvR4` @18:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pYx756KSvR4&t=1085s)
- Do not trade penny stocks for small account strategies. — [`w97KlUrVDk0` @52:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=w97KlUrVDk0&t=3121s)

### A-quality setup only (8)

- Avoid trading B or C quality setups; wait only for A quality setups with perfect chart pattern formation. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @2:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=163s)
- Trade A-quality stocks only; never trade wrong stocks regardless of setup quality. — [`8SWCCRLg1p0` @13:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=8SWCCRLg1p0&t=782s)
- Trade only A+ quality setups; ignore B and C quality setups even if desperate to recover losses. — [`Is9TTagKKjI` @19:50](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Is9TTagKKjI&t=1190s)
- In a bull market you may see five quality setups per day; in a bear market, five per week. — [`_0wygrb5pPs` @42:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_0wygrb5pPs&t=2565s)
- Trade one quality setup per day using full available buying power; maximize profit per trade rather than quantity. — [`l7M03OXCIdA` @7:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=l7M03OXCIdA&t=422s)
- Trade only the highest-quality setups; quality over quantity. — [`oKlhUSSHe2Q` @25:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=oKlhUSSHe2Q&t=1534s)
- STEP 2: Reduce number of trades; only trade A-quality setups in bear market. — [`trcKOZnoPPs` @8:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=trcKOZnoPPs&t=529s)
- During bull market: can take B and C quality setups; in bear: A-quality only. — [`trcKOZnoPPs` @14:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=trcKOZnoPPs&t=847s)

### reversal (9)

- Daily chart must show: recent reverse split, beaten down chart history, stock hitting multi-year or historical low yesterday. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @4:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=278s)
- Reverse split example: 20-to-1 split converts $0.10 stock to $2.00 stock overnight. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @11:09](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=669s)
- Do not try to trade reversals; focus on stocks making big moves up (not down). — [`XqLj6VYzN5c` @23:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=XqLj6VYzN5c&t=1410s)
- Manoli's rule: Trade the theme; capitalize on what's currently moving (pennies, reverse splits). — [`_O0AEVPar7k` @10:58](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_O0AEVPar7k&t=658s)
- Market state/theme awareness: when market is in strong theme (recent reverse splits, Chinese IPOs, etc.), false breakouts less common; when between themes or theme fading, traps increase. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @13:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=786s)
- RSI upper extreme: above 70; lower extreme: below 30. Only trade reversals in these extreme zones. — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @2:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=124s)
- Trade reversals when RSI is at extreme levels AND candlestick pattern confirms (e.g., shooting star at top). — [`iS5lvJGMM8E` @7:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iS5lvJGMM8E&t=437s)
- Most big moves will eventually be reversed; look to catch tide as it begins to change. — [`jfe1Zl-5EQI` @2:29](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jfe1Zl-5EQI&t=149s)
- Only trade reversals at extremes (tops/bottoms); dojis/reversals during sideways are not significant. — [`kQ93wnMCMWQ` @5:04](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=kQ93wnMCMWQ&t=304s)

### emotional control (8)

- STEP 1: Acknowledge heightened emotional state after big loss. — [`Hq3sZh5B_l8` @1:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Hq3sZh5B_l8&t=102s)
- Journal every day: track emotions, general feeling, whether you stayed in control. — [`hLtPtEVBBBQ` @26:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=hLtPtEVBBBQ&t=1587s)
- With small size on first loss: accept and move to next trade without emotional escalation. — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @9:59](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=599s)
- Position scale back down after hitting profit thresholds to prevent emotional decisions on give-back. — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @22:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=1358s)
- Journal every trade and emotional state; track triggers. — [`jvC5GUHPl1I` @23:31](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jvC5GUHPl1I&t=1411s)
- Recovery rule 1: acknowledge heightened emotional state after major loss. — [`t9G3SKzmuh4` @30:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=t9G3SKzmuh4&t=1827s)
- Expect to lose 30% of the time; losses are cost of business; detach emotionally from individual losses. — [`taHYs-v3hBQ` @16:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=taHYs-v3hBQ&t=963s)
- Apply emotional restraint and accept loss rather than chase with aggressive oversizing after a defeat. — [`xauLV_WHXgc` @13:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xauLV_WHXgc&t=800s)

### adding to a winner (7)

- Add to winning positions as they strengthen rather than averaging down into losers. — [`KKoApu04ZF8` @17:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=KKoApu04ZF8&t=1061s)
- Scale into winners, not losers. — [`QstdS67Iyv0` @41:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=QstdS67Iyv0&t=2515s)
- Averaging down prohibition: Do not add to losing trades. — [`Wsq8zdtCcis` @39:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Wsq8zdtCcis&t=2340s)
- Scale in by adding shares at key technical levels as breakout confirms (added at 9.50, 9.50, and 10.00). — [`aIqF9OvWYZ4` @9:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=aIqF9OvWYZ4&t=579s)
- Add to position when already profitable in driver's seat (up $1–2 per share, looking for continuation). — [`jpPgEAAVMxY` @4:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=jpPgEAAVMxY&t=240s)
- Phase four: learn to scale into winners. — [`tR-tyjV1WQ0` @38:20](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=tR-tyjV1WQ0&t=2300s)
- Scale in on dips: add 7,000 shares at $4.60, then at $4.75. — [`yBiAZvwpVww` @6:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yBiAZvwpVww&t=363s)

### first hour / market open (3)

- Step 1: Commit to a schedule; market opens 4:00am-8:00pm; focus on specific daily window. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @2:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=150s)
- Opening hours: 9:30am-11:00am; strong movers often continue. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @33:57](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=2037s)
- Start each trading day with meditation (2+ minutes) to get focused and clear mind before market open. — [`yKV3C2DoaFg` @31:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=yKV3C2DoaFg&t=1875s)

### scanner alert (6)

- Price should start under $10 (ideally under $5) to create percentage gains large enough to attract traders and hit scanners. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @1:47](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=107s)
- Use Day Trade Dash scanners or daytrade-dash.com to find leading gappers and gainers. — [`9VAKgj1Px3s` @2:39](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9VAKgj1Px3s&t=159s)
- Use stock scanners/screeners to find candidates. — [`N3E5eYS8d-I` @6:51](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=N3E5eYS8d-I&t=411s)
- Stock scanners use level one data to identify new highs. — [`SrYGENemM1w` @2:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SrYGENemM1w&t=136s)
- Step 1: Use scanners to identify continuation candidates (stocks on day 2/3 of multi-day uptrend). — [`_RRClNoHamw` @6:02](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_RRClNoHamw&t=362s)
- Trade leading gainers in the market (use scanners to identify them). — [`fOvtzvA923U` @15:34](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=fOvtzvA923U&t=934s)

### daily chart context (2)

- First: examine daily chart looking for wick highs and historical performance (act like detective). — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @0:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=48s)
- Multi-timeframe alignment: both five-minute, one-minute, and daily charts must confirm the trade (perfect storm setup). — [`m12FrF4rrOg` @9:17](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=m12FrF4rrOg&t=557s)

### break-even stop (2)

- Recognize you are treading water when green and red days cancel out, indicating break-even phase. — [`Bqo2XGDZtVU` @5:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Bqo2XGDZtVU&t=303s)
- Danny: When down 2-3 hundred dollars, count it as breakeven. — [`_O0AEVPar7k` @5:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_O0AEVPar7k&t=335s)

### hotkeys (4)

- Do not use hotkeys without offsets; orders will not fill at intended levels. — [`2kMgCjsmFzY` @12:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kMgCjsmFzY&t=723s)
- Purpose of hotkeys is speed — ability to get in and out of positions quickly. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @0:27](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=27s)
- Test hotkeys with small size before live trading to avoid errors in mapping. — [`9hgpZMvCY08` @8:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=9hgpZMvCY08&t=523s)
- Use limit orders by default with NASDAQ route; configure custom order hotkeys to minimize typing. — [`Q-7eBHjD1ks` @38:46](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Q-7eBHjD1ks&t=2326s)

### first candle to make a new high (2)

- Trade only front-side of move (when stock making new highs); do not overtrade backside. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @36:41](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2201s)
- When stock breaks to new high, take quick partial profits; do not hold for full daily range. — [`SpnidshD8Zk` @15:38](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SpnidshD8Zk&t=938s)

### profit-loss ratio 2:1 (2)

- Trader intuition and experience: learn stock personality/behavior; if it false-breakouts once or twice, it will likely do it again. — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @2:30](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=150s)
- Double-bottom vs. double-top: if dips and bounces twice at same level (double bottom), second bounce opportunity often stronger than first (shorts covering). — [`dGhN8uCaMIk` @17:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=dGhN8uCaMIk&t=1065s)

### level 2 / time and sales (2)

- Trick 2: Print out your favorite chart patterns and tape them next to your monitor for visual reference. — [`SmSOboqGPgs` @8:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=SmSOboqGPgs&t=481s)
- Green-on-tape = orders executing at or ABOVE ask (buyers aggressive). Red = at or BELOW bid (sellers aggressive). White = between spread. — [`iBNcF_lPBIg` @39:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=iBNcF_lPBIg&t=2394s)

### PDT rule (2)

- If you have less than $25,000, you're limited to three day trades every five days. — [`y_Qp_ev2js8` @7:22](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y_Qp_ev2js8&t=442s)
- Once account balance exceeds $25,000, you can trade before 90-day freeze period ends. — [`y_Qp_ev2js8` @17:43](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=y_Qp_ev2js8&t=1063s)

### micro pullback (2)

- Trade micro pullbacks only during peak volatility in the beginning of the move, not after consolidation. — [`1FKu4LH0Xss` @34:35](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=1FKu4LH0Xss&t=2075s)
- Use 10-second and 5-minute chart together: 10-second for micro pullbacks, 5-minute for context on extension. — [`HMaTY5-x2N0` @18:00](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=HMaTY5-x2N0&t=1080s)

### uncategorised (342)

- Step 4: Review metrics regularly using trade analytics (Trader View $49/month recommended). — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @24:26](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1466s)
- Phase 2 simple trading plan: momentum trading of leading percentage gainers on front side of move only (not backside). — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @31:13](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=1873s)
- Beginner rule: game of survive to thrive; small green days are good; this is marathon not sprint. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @37:10](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2230s)
- Introduce two trades per day minimum 10 days after one-trade success. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @48:45](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2925s)
- Increase to three trades minimum 10 days; do not cap trade count until suffered drawdown and recovered. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @49:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2951s)
- Cushion technique for multiple trades: start with quarter-size position; increase full size only after +$0.20/share profit. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @49:33](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=2973s)
- This keeps red days smaller (quarter size) while maintaining green day profits. — [`-CqME4wlzVs` @52:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-CqME4wlzVs&t=3127s)
- Core strategy principle: Focus on consistently hitting base hits (small wins), not homerun trades. — [`-N1qf9G3k5c` @2:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-N1qf9G3k5c&t=121s)
- Do not jump from small size (100 shares) to massive size (10,000 shares) immediately; scale gradually. — [`-N1qf9G3k5c` @7:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-N1qf9G3k5c&t=421s)
- Build a cushion early in the week: ease into trading on Monday with smaller profits, step up Tuesday, peak performance Wednesday and Thursday, reduce risk Friday. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @1:11](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=71s)
- Track your metrics and analyze trading data. Identify which months, days of week, and stock price ranges produce your best results. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @12:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=721s)
- Check SEC filings for recent shelf registrations and warrant data to assess dilution risk. — [`016ibIK58t0` @10:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=016ibIK58t0&t=606s)
- Evaluate company cash on balance sheet; if cash insufficient for operations, watch for secondary offerings. — [`016ibIK58t0` @14:23](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=016ibIK58t0&t=863s)
- Avoid trading stocks below $5 per share when using leverage (limits buying power efficiency). — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @4:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=295s)
- Do not take second trade of day if first trade consumed all available quality opportunities. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @21:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=1284s)
- Break of dollar price: watch for move from sub-$1 to $1+; triggers 1-cent spread vs fractional penny spreads below $1 (improves liquidity). — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @3:44](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=224s)
- Take partial profits on breakouts; don't hold full position waiting for extended move. — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @6:55](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=415s)
- Do NOT keep adding shares to losing position (avoid "stubborn" adding = compounding losses). — [`3ovOiVIJ_aM` @7:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3ovOiVIJ_aM&t=421s)
- Day trading is capitalizing on intraday volatility; requires stocks to be moving significantly (10%+ per day). — [`3rEakODkiEg` @1:49](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=109s)
- Focus on stocks up more than 10% as primary candidates; stocks moving outside normal 4% daily range. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @2:37](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=157s)
- Scan market for leading percentage gainers; position 1-3 are usually the best candidates. — [`3rEakODkiEg` @4:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=246s)
- After identifying pattern, check: Is it the right stock? Can I manage risk? Where is the pattern setup? — [`3rEakODkiEg` @39:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=2341s)
- When going live with real money: start small (100 shares), prove consistency for a month, then gradually increase (150, 200 shares per week). — [`45PHATChEJU` @8:07](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=45PHATChEJU&t=487s)
- During trader rehab (August of prior year): scaled by approximately 2x per increment weekly (200 → 400 → 600 → 800 → 1000 → 1500). — [`45PHATChEJU` @8:15](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=45PHATChEJU&t=495s)
- First objective for momentum trading: find a stock with a trend/uptrend. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @28:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=1681s)
- Trade stocks priced between $2 and $20 as sweet spot (with flexibility to $20-30). — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @27:32](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=1652s)
- Stock price must be up at least 10% on the day. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @31:54](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=1914s)
- When trading first position of the day, use starter size to assess market condition, not full size. — [`5AV2OLWD1gc` @15:21](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5AV2OLWD1gc&t=921s)
- Limit to one or two trades per day as beginner; avoid over-trading. — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @16:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=1008s)
- Trade only leading gappers/gainers each day; ignore choppy, narrow-range stocks. — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @8:05](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=485s)
- _...312 more._

## Named setups, by how many videos discuss them

- **42x** bull flag — stock squares up, pulls back in consolidation, breaks higher [00:34:19]
- **27x** micro pullback — Small pullback in strong uptrend; entry on first new high. [00:02:00]
- **18x** abcd pattern — four-point reversal pattern, lower conviction for small account trading. [00:38:11]
- **17x** shooting star — Topping tail then closes red; reversal beginning. [00:21:24]
- **11x** gravestone doji — Ominous pattern with upper wick; price squeezed up then sellers pulled it down. [00:15:04]
- **10x** cup and handle — Double-top U-shape consolidation with handle breakout. [00:45:36]
- **10x** gap and go — Stock gaps up at open on news, pulls back to 9-EMA, then squeezes higher. [00:04:53]
- **10x** dip and rip — stock makes strong move up, pulls back slightly, then squeezes up again to new high; buy the dip for the rip. [00:00:07]
- **9x** dragonfly doji — Has lower wick; bulls rallied it back after weakness. [00:15:20]
- **8x** hammer — Red or green candle with bottoming tail; hammering out support base. [00:18:02]
- **8x** topping tail — upper candle wick showing sellers pulled price back down; bearish signal [00:14:29]
- **7x** bull flag pattern — two to three candles up, two to three down, two to three back up; typical entry. [00:09:03]
- **7x** spinning top — Similar to doji; indicates indecision and reversal likelihood when at extremes. [00:03:57]
- **7x** inverted head and shoulders — pullback followed by higher pivot, then break through resistance; seen on EDBL. [00:07:38]
- **6x** doji — Opens and closes at nearly the same price; signals battle between buyers and sellers. [00:13:08]
- **6x** candle over candle — One candle breaks the high of previous candle; bullish formation. [00:19:04]
- **6x** ascending support line — series of higher lows that can act as support on pullback. [00:29:06]
- **6x** hammer candle — lower wick at bottom of sell-off with small body; bullish reversal at support. [00:37:11]
- **6x** double top — two highs at similar level creating supply zone resistance [00:12:19].
- **6x** pullback pattern — Big candles up, smaller candles, pullback, doji/bottoming tail, then candle over candle. [00:23:36]
- **5x** first pullback entry — buy dip on stock that squeezed up initially. [00:26:28]
- **5x** flat top breakout — stock consolidates near high, breaks through resistance with volume. [00:10:55]
- **5x** double bottom — stock pulls back to near same level twice before resuming uptrend. [00:28:27]
- **5x** momentum trading — Buy stocks moving strongly up on catalyst (news, earnings, partnership); hold minutes to hours for profit. [00:14:23]
- **5x** bottoming tail — long lower wick indicating demand came in at that level.
- **5x** micro pullback strategy — When stock squeezes higher then pulls back, buy first candle that makes new high for bounce move. [00:26:03]
- **4x** sympathy momentum — low-float recent reverse-split follows larger catalyst move in similar stock. [00:08:14]
- **4x** flat-top breakout — consolidation at highs, break through previous day resistance with volume. [00:05:24]
- **4x** daily breakout — break of prior day high on intraday chart, targeting move through whole dollar level. [00:31:50]
- **4x** doji candle — opens and closes at same price; indicates indecision at resistance level [00:28:38]
- **4x** long-body candle — very tall green candle signals strong bullish sentiment and momentum building. [00:33:45]
- **4x** tweezer top — two topping tails back-to-back; resistance, price cannot go higher. [00:38:01]
- **4x** dogee candle — opens and closes at same price showing indecision/battle; gravestone dogee (upper wick) at top is bearish, dragonfly dogee (lower wick) at bottom is bullish [00
- **4x** long-legged doji — very long wicks in both directions; extreme indecision. [00:04:13]
- **4x** continuation setup — Stock with strong prior day continuing higher on Monday and subsequent days. [00:14:44]
- **3x** micro-pullback entry — Sharp pop up, pullback to 80 cents, then bounces higher. Repeat pattern up. [00:46:10]
- **3x** double-top breakout — Two failed attempts at resistance; break above = entry. [00:52:50]
- **3x** macd crossover — 12-period and 26-period moving average convergence/divergence flipping from negative to positive indicates trend reversal and entry signal. [00:01:00]
- **3x** opening range breakout — Break of opening range high (related setup) [00:17:47]
- **3x** red to green move — Stock dips into red then breaks back to green [00:17:42]

- Float under 20 million shares is ideal. — [`-qxkS9Y2zUU` @10:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=-qxkS9Y2zUU&t=640s)

- Trade 2 Day 6: 500 shares at $4.20, float 2.9 million, stock up 40%. — [`2O1r_ICOnTI` @5:24](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2O1r_ICOnTI&t=324s)
- Stock selection criteria: price $2–$20, up minimum 10%, relative volume 5x+, news headline, float under 20M shares. — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @31:48](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=1908s)
- Float on A (ADIL) was 640,000 shares (confirmed via market data vendor). — [`2n2Jt0PEPss` @34:56](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2n2Jt0PEPss&t=2096s)

- Float threshold: under 20 million shares (lower is better for big percentage moves). — [`3rEakODkiEg` @12:01](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3rEakODkiEg&t=721s)
- Float less than 10 million shares preferred. — [`4Pc_von1wS4` @12:03](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4Pc_von1wS4&t=723s)

- Float threshold: under 10 million shares preferred. — [`4t3GDiAXW18` @20:40](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4t3GDiAXW18&t=1240s)
- PIK float: 7.6 million shares (low float). — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @9:52](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=592s)
- Rule of thumb: stocks typically under $20 price, under 20 million share float. — [`5X_ZcifasBg` @11:42](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5X_ZcifasBg&t=702s)
- EVAX float: 3.37 million shares. — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @1:16](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=76s)

- Float precision: 869,000 shares (near end of trading day). — [`5qu6YfbC7tM` @12:06](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5qu6YfbC7tM&t=726s)

### relative volume (x) (82)
